pallavar kālattilum amiccar iruntaṉar. (R. kōpālaṉ, kāñcip pallavar, pak 38.) makēntiravarmaṉ āṭciyil amiccar iruntaṉar eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kūṟiyuḷḷār. (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, ce. 90.) kōmāṟañ caṭiyaṟku uttara mantiri kaḷakkuṭi vittiyaṉ mūvēnta maṅkalap pēraṟiyaṉ ākiya māṟaṅkāri ik kaṟṟaḷi ceytu nīrttaḷiyātē cuvarkkarōkaṇañ ceyta piṉṉi, avaṉukku aṉujaṉ uttara mantrapatam eytiṉa  pāṇṭiya maṅkala viciyatariyaṉ ākiya māṟaṉ eyiṉaṉ mukamaṇṭapañ ceytu nīrttaḷittāṉ (E.I.VII p. 317.) eṉpatu maturi māvaṭṭattu yāṉimalik kalveṭṭuc ceyti. ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta pāṇṭiyaṉ māṟaṉ caṭiyaṉ āṭciyil māṟaṉkāri eṉpavaṉ mutalamiccaṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉukkuppiṉ avaṉ tampiyāṉa māṟaṉ eyiṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ mutalamiccaṉā yiruntāṉ eṉpati ikkalveṭṭiliruntu nām aṟiyalām. ki.pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntavarākak karutappaṭum māṇikkavācakar pāṇṭiyaṉiṭam amiccarāka iruntār eṉpatit tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam terivikkiṟatu.
piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil ceyyappaṭṭa kaliṅkattup paraṇi amiccarkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ mantira pārakar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. (kaṇṇi, 327.) kaliṅka aracaṉukku eṅkarāyaṉ eṉṟa amiccaṉ iruntāṉ eṉṟu atē paraṇi kūṟukiṉṟatu. vikkirama cōḻaṉuṭaṉ amiccarum ulā vantaṉar eṉṟu oṭṭakkūttar kuṟittuḷḷār. periyapurāṇa āciriyarākiya cēkkiḻār anāpaya cōḻaṉukku mutalamiccarāy iruntār eṉac cēkkiḻār purāṇam ceppukiṟatu. (kaṇṇi 74.)
cōḻar kālattil pallavarāyar eṉṟa mutalamiccar cila paṭikaḷukkum talivarāka iruntār.
vicaya nakara āṭciyilum amiccarkaḷ iruntaṉar eṉpatik kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟiyalām. oru nāṭṭu āṭciyil ettuṇi amiccar iruntaṉar eṉpati aṟiyat teḷivāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ illi.
nāyakkar kālattil mutalamiccaṉ piratāṉi, taḷavāy eṉṟa peyarkaḷip peṟṟiruntāṉ. mutalamiccaṉ aracaṉatu ālōcaṉik kuḻuvil mutaliṭam peṟṟiruntāṉ. āṭciyilum mutaliṭattip peṟṟiruntavaṉ avaṉē. avaṉ āṭciyiṉ pala tuṟikaḷik kavaṉittu vantāṉ. (cattiyanātiyar, maturi nāyakkar.)
uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷ
uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷ eṉṟa toṭar cōḻar kalveṭṭukkaḷil aṭikkaṭi varukiṉṟatu. uṭaṉ kūṭṭam eṉṟa toṭari nōkkumpoḻutu, aracaṉuṭaṉ iruntu āṭcit tuṟiyil ceyalāṟṟum kūṭṭam, allatu yōcaṉi kūṟic ceyalāṟṟum kūṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷalām. atirācēntiraṉ kālattut tiruppācūrk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷiyum vēṟu cilar uyar aluvalar peyarkaḷiyum kuṟikkiṉṟatu. tirumukkūṭal kalveṭṭu oṉṟu aracaṉatu āṇiyik kūṟi, anta āṇiyil uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷ aṟuvar kiyeḻuttiṭṭatākavum kūṟukiṉṟatu. (113 of 1930; Epigraphia Indica 21, p. 221, 234, 244,) uṭaṉkūṭṭattu icivum, nāṭu eṉṉum pēraviyiṉ icivum peṟṟē pallavarāyar iraṇṭām irācāti rācaṉukku muṭi cūṭṭiṉār eṉṟu paṭṭayam kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  (113 of 1930; Eigraphia Indica 21  p. 190, 192.)
uṭaṉ kūṭṭattic cērnta nilavari vacūlikkum ilākkāvip paṟṟiya kuṟippu mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. (429 of 1916) ivi aṉittiyum nōkka, cōḻap pēraviyil naṭipeṟṟa aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷil uṭaṉ kūṭṭam toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. aracāṅkattiṉ pala tuṟikaḷiliruntu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa cārpāḷariṉ (pirati nitikaḷiṉ) ālōcaṉik kuḻuvāka aḵtu irukkalām; aḵtu aracaṉukkum, amiccaravi- avartam ilākkākkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkum iṭiyil iruntu toṇṭāṟṟi irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. (Dr. Mahalingam, S.I. polity,p. 131.)
cēṉit talivaṉ
cēṉit talivaṉ āṭcik kuḻuvil iṭam peṟṟiruntāṉ. piṟkālac cōḻaṉ paṭittalivaṉ taṇṭa nāyakaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ; taṇṭam eṉṟa col paṭi, nīti, āṭci purital eṉṟa poruḷkaḷi uṭiyatu. āvūr uṭiyār eṉpavaṉ oru taṇṭa nāyakaṉ. avaṉ jayaṅ koṇṭa cōḻa maṇṭalatti āṇṭu vantāṉ eṉṟu cōḻar kalveṭṭut terivikkiṉṟatu. (39 of 1921.) accol paṭit talivaṉiyum cila iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
purōkitaṉ
caṅka kālattil ācāṉ (purōkitaṉ) aimperuṅkuḻuvil iṭam peṟṟiruntāṉ. piṟkālattil purōkitaṉ iṭam peṟṟatākat teriyavilli. camayat tuṟiyilum oḻukkat tuṟiyilum purōkitaṉ ālōcaṉi kēṭkappaṭṭatu pōlum! piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil purōkitariṉ iṭattil irācakuru amarntiruntāṉ. avaṉatu collukku aracaṉ mikunta matippu vittiruntāṉ eṉpatu kalveṭṭukkaḷāl terikiṉṟatu. mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ āṇiyic cuvāmi tēvar eṉṟa irācakuru māṟṟiviṭṭār eṉṟu kalveṭṭut terivikkiṉṟatu. nāṭṭiṉ camayattip pātukākkat tarmātikāri eṉṟu oruvaṉ tañci makārāṭṭirar kālattil iruntāṉ. (kē.ār. cuppiramaṇiyar, tañci makārāṭṭira maṉṉarkaḷ, pak. 88.)
ceyalāḷaṉ
āṭcik kuḻuviṉ ceyalāḷaṉ uḷpaṭu karumat talivaṉ eṉap paḻaṅkālattil peyar peṟṟāṉ. (ṭākṭar cōmacuntara pāratiyār, tiruvaḷḷuvar.) cōḻaṉ  kālattil tiruvāyk kēḷvi eṉṟu peyar paṭitta aluvalaṉ ceyalāḷaṉāka iruntirukkalām. vijaya nakara kālattil avaṉ irāyacam eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ. tamiḻakattu maṭaṅkaḷil maṭātipatiyiṭam ceyalāḷarāka iruppavar irāyacam eṉṟē iṉṟum aḻikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉar.
aracāṅka uyar aluvalar
oru nāṭṭu aracāṅkam naṭipeṟat teḷivāṉa ilākkākkaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟil vēlipārkka uyar aluvalarkaḷum eḻuttarkaḷum iruppatu iyalpu. avvaluvalarum cōḻar kalveṭṭukkaḷil karumikaḷ, peruntaram allatu peruntaṉam eṉṟum, paṇi makkaḷ, ciṟutaram allatu ciṟutaṉam eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. ciṟutaṉattup paṇimakkaḷ, ciṟutaṉattu valaṅki vēḷikkārap paṭikaḷ, peruntaṉattuc cēṉātipati srī kiruṣṇaṉ rāmaṉ, peruntaram tirumali veṇkāṭaṉ, peruntaṉattu āṉi āṭkaḷ, peruntaṉattu valaṅki vēḷikkārap paṭikaḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa toṭarkaḷ kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. taṉam eṉpatum taram eṉpatum orē poruḷit tarum coṟkaḷ ātalāl, cēṉātipati pōṉṟa uyartara aluvalar peruntaram eṉṟum, aṭuttu vēli pārppavar ciṟutaram eṉṟum aracāṅka muṟiyil aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamākum. (A.R. E. 1913, 22. ūrmakkaḷiṉ talivaṉ iṉṟum peruntaṉakkāraṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭal iṅku Ãid¡f‰ghyJ.) peruntarattic cērnta uyar aluvalar karumikaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar; ciṟutarattic cērntavarkaḷ paṇimakkaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar. ivviruvakikkum iṭippaṭṭa aluvalar ciṟutaṉattup peruntaṉam eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷalām. (29 of 1897.)
paṭittalivaṉ pōṉṟa uyar aluvalarkku kuṟippiṭṭa ūr allatu nilam māṉiyamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. pala cantarppaṅkaḷil oru kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṉ varuvāy muḻuvatum ōr atikāri peṟumpaṭi aracaṉ āṇiyiṭṭirup pāṉ; cila cantarppaṅkaḷil appakutiyil oru pakuti varuvāyi maṭṭum avaṉ peṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi vitittiruppāṉ. nilam maṭṭum avaṉukkuc contamāyirātu. cila kālaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭa ūrō, perum pakuti nilamō uyar aluvalarkku vaḻaṅkuvatum uṇṭu. avarkaḷ tamakkuk koṭukkappaṭṭa ūriṉ allatu nilattiṉ varuvāyil oru pakutiyi aracaṉukku vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum; pōrkkālattil oru ciṟupaṭiyiyum aracaṉukku utava vēṇṭum. nāyakkarkaḷ āṭciyil pāḷiyakkārar ittakiya muṟiyil iruntaṉar. pēraracarkku aṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracarākiya aracāṅka aluvalarum immuṟiyil iruntaṉar; ittakiya urimi peṟṟamiyāṟṟāṉ kiḻār, nāṭāḷvār, eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar pōlum! ūrō, nilamō tamakku urimiyākap peṟṟavar, tam viruppam pōl avaṟṟic ceytukoḷḷa urimi uṭiyavar āvar. ( K.A.N.rh¤âÇ, cōḻar II, pak241.)
araciyalil paṇiyāṟṟiya uyar aluvalar ēṉāti, mārāyaṉ ( mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻi -tolkāppiyam. mārāyam maṉivi mārāci eṉappaṭṭāṉ.) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟaṉar. ippaṭṭaṅkaḷ pōrt tiṟamiyik kuṟittē mutalil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa; piṉpu kālap pōkkil pōril īṭupaṭāta uyar aluvalarkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. caṅkakālat tamiḻakattil pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉatu peyarit tāṅki avaṉatu araṇmaṉip poṟkollaṉ iruntāṉ eṉpatu cilappatikārattiliruntu terikiṟatu. (teṉṉavaṉ peyaroṭu ciṟappup peṟṟa, poṉviṉik kollaṉ ivaṉ eṉap porunti. 	-cilampu. kāti 16- vari 109- 110.) cōḻar āṭciyil ariyaṉ, pērariyaṉ, irācaṉ, atirācaṉ, piḷḷi, mutali, nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷi uyar aluvalar tāṅkiyiruntaṉar. (S.I. polity p. 137.)irācarācap pirammātirācaṉ eṉṟāṟpōlac cilar tam kālattilirunta aracar peyarkaḷiyum tāṅkiyiruntaṉar.
tiruvāyk kēḷvi: ivaṉ aracaṉuṭaṉ iruntu avaṉ kūṟum kaṭṭaḷikaḷik kēṭṭu eḻutupavaṉ.
tirumantira ōli: ivaṉ, aracaṉ taṉ amiccaraviyil muṭivu ceyta ceytikaḷi eḻutum uyar aluvalaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam eḻutum aracāṅkat tuṟiyiṉ talivaṉ tirumantira ōli nāyakam eṉpavaṉ.
viṭiyil atikāri: ivaṉ aracāṅkat toṭarpāṉa kēḷvikaṭku viṭi eḻutum atikāri. ivaṉukkuk kīḻ oru tuṟi iruntatu.
āṇatti: iṅṅaṉam eḻutappaṭum viṭikaḷi aṉuppum tuṟikkut talivaṉ āṇatti eṉpavaṉ.
aracāṅka āṇikaḷiyum ceppēṭukaḷiyum eḻutat takka tiṟamiyāḷar aṭaṅkiya tuṟi oṉṟum iruntatu. kōvilkaḷi mēṟpārkkum atikārikaḷum iruntaṉar.
nilavari paṟṟiya tuṟikaḷ
puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷam eṉpatu ōr aracāṅkap pirivākum. itu nilavari vacūlikkum tuṟi eṉṟu kūṟalām. ittuṟi pattup pirivukaḷip peṟṟiruntatu. avi:- (1) puravu varit tiṇikkaḷam, (2) varippottakam, (3) puravu varit tiṇikkaḷak kaṇkāṇi, (4) mukaveṭṭi, (5) terippu, (6) taravu cāttu, (7) paḻaniyāyam, (8) variyilīṭu, (variyēṭu), (9) varip puttakak kaṇakku, (10) paṭṭōli eṉpaṉa.
(1) vari vacūlikkum muḻuttuṟiyum, nilavarit toṭarpāṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum mutalil kavaṉikkum tuṟiyum cērnta oṉṟē puravu varit tiṇikkaḷam eṉṟu collalām.
(2) varik kaṇakku vittuḷḷa tuṟi varippottakam eṉappaṭṭatu.
(3) varik kaṇakkip paricīlaṉi ceyyum tuṟi puravu varit tiṇikkaḷak kaṇkāṇi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.
(4) nilam paṟṟiya aracaṉ āṇikaḷic ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum kallilum veṭṭuvōr iruntuvanta tuṟi mukaveṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
(5) nilatti aḷantu pārkkum tuṟi terippu (Survey) eṉappaṭṭatu.
(6) koṭutta paṇattiṟku iracītu koṭukkum tuṟi taravu cāttu eṉappaṭṭatu.
(7) paḻiya pākkikaḷik kavaṉikkum tuṟi paḻa niyāyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
(8) varipputtakattil varikaḷip pativu ceyyum tuṟi variyilīṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
(9) varipputtakattiluḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷik kavaṉikkum tuṟi varipputtakak kaṇakku eṉappaṭṭatu.
(10) aracaṉ āṇikaḷip pātukākkum tuṟi paṭṭōli eṉappaṭṭatu. (A.R.E. 1931- 32, 14.)
āvaṇak kaḷari
āvaṇam - pattiram. kaḷari- aluvalakam. annāḷil ūrkaḷtōṟum eḻutappaṭum āvaṇaṅkaḷi (pattiraṅkaḷik) kāppiṭa (pativu ceyya) āvaṇak kaḷariyum (Registration office) iruntatu. nilatti viṟpōrum vāṅkuvōrum āvaṇattuṭaṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟu nilattiṉ viliyiyum nāṉkelliyiyum terivittut tam uṭaṉpāṭṭiṟkum uṟutimoḻi kūṟi āvaṇam kāppiṭap peṟṟa piṉṉart tirumpuvar. ivvāvaṇam eṉṟum payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyiruppatāyiruppiṉ, avvūriluḷḷa kōyiṟ cuvaril ataṉip poṟittu vippatu vaḻakkam. (T.V.S. paṇṭārattār, mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉ, pak. 93)
aracāṅka vēlikaḷi mēṟpārkka uyar aluvalar nāṭu muḻumiyum cuṟṟuvatuṇṭu. aracarkaḷum tam aracāṅka naṭavaṭikkikaḷi aṟivataṟkāka avvappoḻutu nāṭu cuṟṟivaruvatuṇṭu.

5. varavu celavu
varavukkuriya vaḻikaḷ
(1) nilavari, (2) cottuvari, (3) aluvalvari, (4) vaṇika vari (5) toḻilvari, (6) paṭikkuriya varumāṉam, (7) camutāya- kuḻuvarikaḷ, (8) nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷil vitikkum taṇṭappaṇam, (9) kāṭṭu varumāṉam, curaṅka varumāṉam, uppaḷa varumāṉam, nātiyaṟṟa cottu mutaliyaṉa.
(1) naṉcey, puṉcey eṉṟu payir nilaṅkaḷ iruvakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭu nilavari vitikkappaṭṭatu. avi mā, kuḻi, kāṇi, mukkāṇi, arikkāṇi, muntiriki eṉṟu ciṟṟaḷavilum, vēli eṉṟu pēraḷavilum pēcappaṭṭaṉa. oru vēliyiṉ 1/52, 428. 800 eṉṉum ciṟu pakutiyum cōḻar kālattil kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟu tañcip periya kōvil kalveṭṭut terivikkiṟatu. (A.R.E. 1900, para 25.) āṟṟup pāyccaluḷḷa nilaṅkaḷil uṟpatti mikutiyākavum maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷil kuṟivākavum viḷivip poṟuttu vari vitikkappaṭṭatu.
ovvoru kirāmattilum kōvilkaḷ, toḻilāḷarum paṇṇiyāṭkaḷum vāḻumiṭaṅkaḷ, kuḷaṅkaḷ, maṉṟam, kōvilkaḷic cērnta nantavaṉaṅkaḷ, nel aṭikkum kaḷam, kālnaṭikaḷ mēyum iṭam, āṟṟaṅkariyi aṭutta catuppunilam, uṭippu uṇṭāṉa nilaṅkaḷ, payiriṭa muṭiyāta nilam, iṭukāṭu, cuṭukāṭu eṉpavi vari vitikkappaṭāta iṭaṅkaḷākum.
vari vitikkappeṟṟa nilaṅkaḷ taram peṟṟa nilaṅkaḷ eṉṟum, vari vitikkap peṟātavi, taramili nilaṅkaḷ eṉṟum cōḻar kālattil peyar peṟṟaṉa. (A.R.E. 1935- 36, para 43.) oru vēliyiṉ irupatil oru pākam mā eṉappaṭum. oru māvukku ivvaḷavu eṉṟu vari vitikkappaṭṭatu. nilavari kaṭami eṉa oru kālattil peyar peṟṟatu. nāṟpatu kalam viḷiyum oru mā nilattiṟku 3 kalam variyāka vāṅkappaṭṭatu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil mikutiyākavum vāṅkappaṭṭatu.
cōḻar kālattil nilaṅkaḷ yāvum aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. mutal irācarācaṉ kālattilum, mutal kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattilum, vikkirama cōḻaṉ kālattilum, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattilum nilaṅkaḷ aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. nilam aḷanta kōlkaḷ 12, 14, 16, 20 aṭi nīḷamuḷḷavi. (229 of 1910, 261 of 1902, 413 of 1922, 104 of 1928) iviyallāmal kaṭikik kaḷattuk kōl, srīpātak kōl, māḷikik kōl eṉṟu vēṟu aḷavukōlkaḷum iruntaṉa. (160 of 1921, 87 of 1900,99 of 1913) nilam aḷakka aracāṅka ilākā oṉṟu iruntatu. mutal irācarācaṉ kālattil anta ilākāviṉ talivaṉ, cēṉātipati kuravaṉ irācarāca makārācaṉ eṉpavaṉ, avaṉ nilam aḷantatāl ulakaḷantāṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ. (A.R.E. 1913, para 21)
nilavari āṟil oru pakuti aracāṅkam vacūlittatu eṉpatu potukkūṟṟu. pala iṭaṅkaḷil pala kālaṅkaḷil nāṉkil oru paṅkum mūṉṟil oru paṅkum iraṇṭil orupaṅkum aracāṅkattiṟkuc ceṉṟu vantatu.
(2) cottuvari
ikkālattil oru maṉitaṉatu āṇṭu varuvāyik koṇṭē, cottu varuvāy vitikkappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaṅ kālattil avaṉatu varuvāyippaṟṟi aracāṅkam kavalip paṭavilli; avaṉatu cottiṉmītē vari vitikkappaṭṭatu. vīṭukaḷ, maṉikaḷ, pacukkaḷ, erutukaḷ, āṭukaḷ, vaṇṭikaḷ, kutirikaḷ, nakikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmītu vari vitikkappaṭṭatu. (A.R.E. 1915, para 44; 1917; para 47; 1921,para 41 etc.)
(3) aluval vari
ovvoruvaṉum ceytu vanta toḻilukkum attoḻilukku avaṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvikkum vari vitikkappaṭṭatu. kuyavaṉ tāṉ ceyta toḻilukkum, payaṉ paṭuttiya tirikikkum vari celuttiṉāṉ. avvāṟē calavit toḻilāḷi taṉ toḻilukkum tāṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya kalliṟkum vari celuttiṉāṉ. ivvāṟē ovvoru toḻilāḷiyum vari celuttiṉāṉ. vaṇṇāṉ kallukkuc celuttiya vari pallavar kālattil pāṟik kāṇam eṉṟum, cōḻar kālattil vaṇṇār pāṟi eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu. avaṉ toḻilukkuc celuttiya vari kallāyam eṉappaṭṭatu. kaḷ iṟakkiyavaṉ taṉ toḻilukkum, tāṉ payaṉ paṭuttiya ēṇikkum vari celuttiṉāṉ. (A.R.E. 1910,para 54.) inta aluval varikaḷ nāṇayamākavē celuttappaṭṭaṉa. oru kuṭumpattil uḷḷa ellōrum oru toḻilil īṭupaṭṭiruntāl avar aṉivarumē vari celuttak kaṭamippaṭṭavar eṉpatu akkāla vitiyākum. (294 of 1910.) tamiḻakattil taṅki irunta piṟa nāṭṭārum vari celuttiṉar. yāttiri ceytavarum vari celuttiṉar. piḷḷi vari, āḷvari, pēr kaṭami eṉṟa varikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. (Ins of pudukkotti state, 711, 733, 784)
(4) vaṇika vari
ōriṭattil viṟpataṟku iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiṉmītu vitikkappaṭṭa vari tala ātāyam eṉappaṭṭatu. oru māvaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyāka viṟpaṉikkuriya poruḷkaḷ aṉuppappaṭumpōtu vitikkappaṭṭa vari mārkka ātāyam eṉappaṭṭatu. ayal nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷukku vitikkappaṭṭa vari māmūl ātāyam eṉappaṭṭatu. mārkka ātāyam eṉpatu māvaṭṭa ellikaḷil allatu nakara ellikaḷil amikkap paṭṭirunta cuṅkaccāvaṭikaḷil vacūlikkappaṭṭa varippaṇamākum. oru nakarattiliruntu vēṟu māvaṭṭattiṟkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuk kākap pala cuṅkac cāvaṭikaḷil vari kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam akkālattil iruntatu. ataṉāl kuṭimakkaḷ mikavum tuṉpuṟṟaṉar. ataṉāl mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ iccuṅka variyi nīkkiṉāṉ; cuṅkam tavirtta kulōttuṅkaṉ eṉṟu kuṭimakkaḷāl pārāṭṭappeṟṟāṉ (374 of 1908)
(5) toḻil vari
uppu uṇṭākkiyatip poṟuttum, iṟakkappaṭṭa kaḷḷip poṟuttum, akkālattil vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. taṟi, cekku, marakkalam, paṭaku mutaliya toḻil niliyaṅkaḷ mītu vitikkappaṭṭa variyē toḻilvari eṉappaṭṭatu.
(6) paṭikkuriya varumāṉam
mikap piṟpaṭṭa nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil paṭiyip pātukāppataṟkup potumakkaḷiṭamiruntu aracāṅkam vari vacūlittatu. avvari paṭṭayaka kāṇikki, taṇṭanāyakar makami eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa. (E.I.7. p. 304; 510 of 1921.) kōṭṭikaḷip pātukākka vacūlikkappaṭṭa paṇam kōṭṭip paṇam, kōṭṭip pativu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa. (A.R.E. 1917. 97; 1922,para 43.)
mutaṟ parāntakaṉ kuṭamūkkuc capiyārkku mūvāyiram kaḻañcu poṉ taṇṭam vitittu, ataṉip pāṇṭip paṭikkuc celuttumpaṭik kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu.  (255 of 1911.) oru vēli nilattiṟku oru kaḻañcu poṉ vītam ālaṅkuṭi makkaḷ taṉ vēṅki nāṭṭup pōrukkuc celuttavēṇṭum eṉṟu vīrarācēntiraṉ kaṭṭaḷi piṟappittāṉ. (521 of 1920.) iṅṅaṉam pōrk kālaṅkaḷil taṉivari vacūlikkappaṭṭateṉpatu ivaṟṟāl terikiṟataṉṟō?
iravu eṉpatu oru varikkup peyar. aracaṉ cila camayaṅkaḷil pōr allatu pañcam nērntapoḻutu- kuṭimakkaḷiṭam poruḷ utavikku irantāṉpōlum! appoḻutu kuṭimakkaḷ celuttiya paṇam iravu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalām. (K.A.N. cāttiri, cōḻar, pak. 522.)
7. camutāya- kuḻuvarikaḷ
valaṅkic cātiyiṉariṭamum, iṭaṅkic cātiyiṉariṭamum piṟa vakuppiṉariṭamum oruvaki vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. atu paṭṭiṟi, paṭṭaṟi, (kuḻu varikaḷ), cammātam (patiṉeṭṭuc cātikaḷmītu vari) eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. (373 of 1916, 30 of 1913, 221 of 1910. ) kōvil naṭi muṟikku eṉṟu taṉivari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. tirumaṇattiṟkum, tirumaṇa ūrvalaṅ kaḷukkum taṉittaṉi varikaḷ vacūlikkappaṭṭaṉa.
nītimaṉṟattil vitikkappaṭṭa taṇṭap paṇam maṉṟupāṭu, taṇṭam, mutaliya peyarkaḷip peṟṟatu. paṭṭiyil aṭikkappaṭum caṇṭik kālnaṭikaḷukkum taṇṭam vitikkappaṭṭatu. (A.R. E. 1920, para 79.)
kāṭṭu varumāṉam mutaliyaṉavum aracāṅkattic cērntaṉavēyākum.
palavaki varikaḷ
variyip potuvākak kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ āyam, iṟi, kaṭami eṉpaṉa. palavakic ciṟu varikaḷ ciṟṟāyam, cilliṟi eṉṟa peyaril vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ūr, capi, nakaram eṉpavi vititta varikaḷ ūriṭu varippāṭu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. aracāṅkattukkuc celuttavēṇṭiya varikaḷ aṉittiṟkum kuṭimi eṉṟa potuppeyar iruntatu. (K.A.N. cāttiri, cōḻar, pak. 522- 123.)
vari vacūlikkum muṟikaḷ
aracāṅkam tāṉ vititta variyit tāṉiyamākavum, paṇamākavum vacūlittatu. tāṉiyamāka vacūlittal nel āyam, nel mutal eṉappeyar peṟṟatu; piṉṉatu kācāyam, poṉ mutal eṉappeyar peṟṟatu; piṉṉatu kācāyam, poṉ mutal eṉappeyar peṟṟatu; cōḻar kālattil nilavari tāṉiyamākavē vāṅkappaṭṭatu. avvāṟu vāṅkappaṭṭa tāṉiyam ovvoru kirāmattilum irunta aracāṅkak kutirkaḷil cērkkappaṭṭatu. ēṉiya varikaḷ paṇamākavē vacūlikkappaṭṭaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil nilavariyum paṇamākavē vacūlikkappaṭṭatu.
vari vāṅkum muṟikaḷ
kuṭikaḷiṭam varikaḷi vacūlikka ēṟattāḻa nāṉku muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa: (1) aracāṅkam ōr ilākāviyē amittu aracāṅka aluvalarik koṇṭu nērē variyi vacūlittal; (2) aracāṅkattiṟkum kuṭikaḷukkum iṭiyil oru kuḻuviṉar iruntu varikaḷi vacūlittal; (3) kuṟippiṭṭa nilappakutiyiṉ varuvāyi oruvariṭam oppaṭittu, ataṉ oru pakutiyi aracāṅkattiṭam āṇṭutōṟum koṭukkac ceytal; (4) oru nāṭṭip pala pakutikaḷākap pirittu ovvoru pakutiyiyum oru talivaṉiṭam oppaṭittal; attalivaṉ āṇṭutōṟum kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyi aracaṉukkuc celuttuvatōṭu pōrkkālattil paṭi utaviyum ceyvatāka oppukkoṇṭu naṭattal. (S.I. polity, p. 177.)
(1) aracaṉukkuriya nilaṅkaḷuḷḷa kirāmaṅkaḷil akkirāmat talivarkaḷ uḻavarkaḷiṭam vari vacūlittu aracāṅkattiṟku aṉuppuvar. cila kirāma nilaṅkaḷ uḻavarāl viḷikkappaṭṭaṉa; kirāmat talivarkaḷāl vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu.
(2) ūravi allatu capi irunta kirāmaṅkaḷil anta aviyiṉar allatu capiyiṉar caṭṭappaṭi varikaḷi vacūlittu aracāṅkattiṟkuc celutti vantaṉar.
(3) kuttaki, kaṭṭuk kuttaki, cittāyam (ciṟṟāyam?) eṉṟa muṟiyil cila kirāmaṅkaḷ cilariṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyi aracāṅkam peṟṟu vantatu.
(4) cōḻap perunāṭṭuc ciṟṟaracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nilappakutikaḷil varikaḷi vacūlittaṉar; oru pakutiyi naṭu aracāṅkattukku aṉuppiṉar; maṟṟoru pakutiyit taṅkaḷ āṭcikku vittukkoṇṭaṉar. ittalivarkaḷ āṇṭu tōṟum ivvāṟu kappam celutti vantaṉar. maturi nāyakkar āṭciyil ivvāṟē pāḷiyakkārarkaḷ iruntu vantārkaḷ. (S.I. polity, pak. 177- 180)
vari vitipput tuṟi
aracāṅka varaviyum celaviyum kavaṉikkap poruḷ tuṟi (ilākā) oṉṟu taṉiyē iyaṅki vantatu. caṅka kālattil anta ilākāvil kāvitikaḷ (variyilār) eṉpavar uyar aluvalarāka iruntaṉar eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. kāviti eṉpavaṉ poruḷ amiccaṉ. avaṉukkuk kīḻ āyak kaṇakkar eṉpavar vēli pārttaṉar. (cilap. kāti 22 - vari9; kāti 28- vari 205. kāviti - variyilār eṉpar arumpatavuriyāciriyar. āyam- varuvāy- kuṟaḷ 933) āyam- varuvāy. piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil puravu varittiṇik kaḷam eṉpatu inta ilākāvē ākum. (S.I. polity, p. 181)
cila nilaṅkaḷukku uriya variyi orē tokiyākac celuttiviṭiṉ, annilaṅkaḷukku āṇṭutōṟum varivitippa tilli. eṉavē, avi iṟiyili eṉappaṭum. kōvilukkō, maṭattiṟkō, vēṟu tāṉam ceyvataṟkō nilaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvōr annilaṅkaḷukkuriya tokiyiyum variyiyum orē muṟiyil celutti viṭuvatu vaḻakkam. anta varit tokiyiliruntu āṇṭutōṟum varum vaṭṭippaṇam etir kālattil varikku īṭuceyya muṭiyum. inta muṟiyāl aracāṅkamō ūraviyō variyi iḻakkavilli eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakum. cila pakutikaḷil ivvāṟu aṟaniliyaṅkaḷukku viṭappaṭum nilaṅkaḷukkuriya variyi īṭuceyya, anta ūriluḷḷa maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷukku uyarnta vari vitippatum uṇṭu. (S.I. polity, p. 182)
nāṭṭiṉ cila pakutikaḷil ti mātattil vari vacūlikkap paṭṭatu. peṟṟukkoṇṭa varikku iracītu koṭukkappaṭṭatu. variyil oru pakuti kaṭṭappaṭṭatuṭaṉ, anta āṇṭukkuriya eccam kuṟikkappaṭṭu, ataṉip peṟukiṟa urimiyiyum eḻuttu vaṭivil peṟṟukkoṇṭu, avvāṟē attokiyi vacūlippatu atikārikaḷ poṟuppāka iruntu vantatu. cila pakutikaḷil āṇṭukku mūṉṟu muṟi vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. vicayanakara āṭciyil āṇṭu tōṟum navarāttiri kālattil vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. (S.I. polity, p. 185)
varic cumi
oru māviṟkuk kuṟinta aḷavuḷḷa nilamāyiṉum ataṉkaṇ viḷinta ariciyik kavaḷamākki yāṉikkut taruvatāyiṉ, avvarici ataṟkup pala nāṭkaḷ uṇavākum. māvi viṭap periya viḷinilam āyiṉum, ataṉkaṇ yāṉiyip pukaviṭṭāl, atu uṇṇum aḷaviviṭac cinti nācamākkum aḷavē mikutiyāka irukkum. atu pōlavē aṟivuṭiya aracaṉ kuṭikaḷiṭam aḷavōṭu vari vacūlittāl, avaṉatu aracāṅkap paṇṭāram perukum; avaṉum pārāṭṭappaṭuvāṉ. aracaṉ aṟivaṟṟavaṉāyum, naṉṉeṟiyiṟ cellāta vari vacūlikkum aluvalari uṭi yavaṉākavum, kuṭimakkaḷmītu poṟuppaṟṟa muṟiyil varikaḷic cumattupa vaṉākavum iruntāl, tāṉum anupaviyāmal nāṭṭiyum pāḻpaṭuttiyavaṉ āvāṉ, eṉṟu picirāntiyār pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampikku eṭuttukkūṟiṉār. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 184.)
	vēloṭu niṉṟāṉ iṭueṉ ṟatupōlum
	 kōloṭu niṉṟāṉ toṭarpu
eṉṟa kuṟaṭ karuttu iṅku niṉivu kūrat takum.
paḻaṅkālattil cila cantarppaṅkaḷil koṭiya muṟiyil vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. makēntira maṅkalattu aviyōr cōḻa aracāṅkap paṭiyiṉarāl taṇṇīril aḻuttappaṭṭaṉar; koṭiya veyyilil niṟuttappaṭṭaṉar. piṉpu avarkaḷ tañcāvūr ceṉṟu mutalām irācarācaṉiṭam muṟiyiṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. (159 of 1895) tāṉ varippaṇam pākki illi eṉṟu coṉṉataṟkāka oru peṇmaṇi tuṉpuṟuttappaṭṭāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ viṭatti uṭkoṇṭu iṟantāḷ. avaḷik koṭumippaṭuttiya vari atikāri, akkuṟṟattiṟkāka avvūrkkōvilil nāḷtōṟum taṉ celavil viḷakku eriyat tāṉam ceytāṉ eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. (159 of 1895, 80 of 1906)
cila camayaṅkaḷil aracāṅkattukku vari celutta iyalāmal kuṭikaḷ nilaṅkaḷi viṟṟaṉar. puṉṉi vāyil eṉṟa ūr aviyiṉariṭam muḻuvarit tokiyiyum vacūlikka muṭiyāmiyāl, vari atikāri avarkaḷic ciṟiyiliṭṭāṉ. ataṉ mītu ūraviyiṉar kirāmattic cērnta 80 vēli nilatti irunūṟu paḻaṅkācukaḷukku viṟṟu muḻu variyiyum celuttiviṭṭaṉar. (202 of 1912)
tāṅka muṭiyāta varic cumiyiṉāl cila camayaṅkaḷil kuṭi makkaḷ kotippaṭintu etirppuk kāṭṭuvatum uṇṭu. mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil pacukkaḷ mītum peṇ erumikaḷ mītum tiṭīreṉṟu vari vitikkappaṭṭatu. 18 māvaṭṭaṅkaḷic cērnta valaṅkic cātiyiṉar oṉṟu kūṭiṉar; cōḻar āṭciyiṉ toṭakka kālam mutal cōḻa maṇṭalattu eḻupatteṭṭu nāṭukaḷilum toṇṭi maṇṭalattic cērnta nāṟpatteṭṭāyiram pūmikaḷilum pacukkaḷukkum peṇ erumikaḷukkum vari vitikkappaṭavilli. ātalāl putiya atikāri vititta variyik koṭukka iyalātu; mēlum, puñci viḷiccalil aintil oru pakutiyum kuḷattup pāyccal viḷivil mūṉṟil oru pakutiyum variyāka aracāṅkam peṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum, eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṉar. (464 of 1911) mūṉṟām irācarācaṉ kālattil tañci māvaṭṭattiṉ cila pakutikaḷil aracāṅka atikārikaḷ palar atika varikaḷi vāṅkat toṭaṅkiyatāl, uḻavarkaḷ nalla nili ēṟpaṭum vari nilaṅkaḷil payir viyāmal niṟuttiviṭṭaṉar. itaṉi aṟinta maṉṉārkuṭi aviyiṉarum cuṟṟiyirunta aintu nāṭukaḷiṉ pēraviyiṉarum oṉṟu kūṭiṉar; vaḻakkamāṉa varikaḷi maṭṭumē celutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṉar. (S.I. I. VI. 48, 50, 58)
cila camayaṅkaḷil variccumiyāl valaṅki, iṭaṅkic cātikaḷ aracāṅkatti accuṟuttiṉa. viruttācalap pakutiyil aracāṅka atikārikaḷum nilakkiḻārkaḷum kuṭikaḷit tuṉpuṟuttiṉar. kāṇiyāḻvāṉum pirāmaṇarum varikaḷip peṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, anta iruvakic cātiyiṉarum, nammik koṭumip paṭuttiyavarkaḷukkut taṅka iṭam utavuvatō, avarkaḷukkuk kaṇakku eḻutuvatō, avarkaḷ colvati oppuk koḷvatō kūṭātu; immuṭivukku māṟāka, naṭappavar kollappaṭuvār, eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṉar. (92 of  1918)
atikārikaḷ viḷiccalukku ēṟṟavāṟu vari vitikkāmal mikutiyāka vari vitittamiyāl, nām ūri viṭṭē ōṭa niṉittōm. nammuḷ oṟṟumi illāmiyāl nām koṭumippaṭuttappaṭṭōm. iṉi nām viḷiccalukku ēṟṟapaṭiyē vari celuttuvōm; atikamāka vitittālum koṭōm, eṉṟu tañci māvaṭṭattic cērnta koṟukki (kuṟukki) eṉṉum ūril irunta valaṅki- iṭaṅkic cātiyiṉar tīrmāṉittaṉar. avarkaḷē iṉṉiṉṉa poruḷukku ivvaḷavu vari eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar. koyyā maram, pākkumaram, paṉi maram, vāḻi maram, karumpu, centāmari, muttukkoṭṭic ceṭi, eḷ, puḷi, iñci mutaliyavi payiriṭa vari vitikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu attiṭṭatti liruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. (216 of 1917)
variccumiyāl kuṭimakkaḷ payir ceyvati viṭuttu, ayalūrkaḷil kuṭiyēṟiṉmiyum uṇṭu. aracāṅkam avarkaḷi varunti aḻittu varic cumiyik kuṟit tamikkum cāṉṟukaḷ pala uṇṭu. perunakaril irunta necavāḷar varikaṭṭa iyalātu vēṟu ūril kuṭiyēṟiṉar. vicayanakara aracāṅkam avarkaḷatu variyik kuṟittatu. paḻiya ūril kuṭiyēṟumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (370 of 1923.) teṉ ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattiṉ cila pakutikaḷil valaṅki, iṭaṅkic cātikaḷ mītu mikutiyāka vitikkappaṭṭirunta iṉavari, iṭaṅkivari eṉpavaṟṟic celutta iyalāmal vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉar. itaṉiyaṟinta aracaṉ appakutiyi āṇṭa talivaṉiṭam avvarikaḷi nīkkumpaṭi āṇiyiṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu cila camayaṅkaḷil cila nikaḻccikaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. (476 of 1921.)
aracāṅkac caluki
āṟṟu veḷḷattāl nilaṅkaḷ viḷiccal taṉmiyi iḻantuviṭṭāl, aracāṅkam vēṇṭiya utavi ceyvatu vaḻakkam; mīṇṭum payiriṭat toṭaṅkiya mutalāṇṭil pātivariyit tāṉ vacūlikkum; aṭutta āṇṭu mukkāl pakuti variyi vacūlikkum; paṇamākac celuttum kuṭimi, kāṇikki varikaḷi nīkkiviṭum. paḻiya variyil pātiyum, putuvariyum vacūlikkum; mutalāṇṭil kuṭiyiruppavarkkum kaṭami, aracuppēṟu, vācalpaṇam, āyam, pulvari mutaliya varikaḷil pāti aḷavē mutalāṇṭil vacūlikkum; aṭutta āṇṭu mukkāl variyi vacūlikkum. ivvāṟē kirāmaṅkaḷukkum kōvilkaḷukkum uriya nilaṅkaḷ mītu vari vacūlikkappaṭum. oru kirāmam koḷḷi, paṭiyeṭuppu ivaṟṟāl tuṉpap paṭṭālum itē muṟiyil kuṟinta vari vacūlikkappaṭum. (422 of 1912, 629 of 1923.)
payiriṭappaṭāta iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟip payiriṭumāṟu aracāṅkam makkaḷit tūṇṭuvatumuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam payiriṭap paṇpaṭuttappaṭum nilattiṟkuk kuṟitta kālamvari aracāṅkam vari vitikkātu.
aracāṅkac celavu
perunāṭṭilirunta ciṟṟaracarkaḷ tattam nāṭṭuk kāvaṟpaṭikkum pōrppaṭikkum tāṅkaḷē celavaḻittuk koṇṭaṉar. naṭu aracāṅka atikārikaḷukku avartam aluvalukkāka māṉiyaṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa ovvoru nāṭṭilum (mākāṇattilum) nāṭukāval (pōlī) paṭiyiṉar antanta nāṭṭu varuvāyil paṇiyāṟṟi vantaṉar. ovvōr ūrilum ūrāṭci maṉṟam iruntu vaḻakkukaḷit tīrttu vantamiyāl, naṭu aracāṅkattiṟku ataṟkeṉṟu taṉic celavu ēṟpaṭavilli. ikkālattip pōlap pala vakit tuṟikaḷilum akkāla aracāṅkam poruḷ celaviṭat tēviyilli.
(1) āyiṉum akkāla aracarkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar ōyātu pōriṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpati varalāṟu uṇarttukiṟatu. ātalāl naṭu aracāṅkam taṉatu varuvāyiṉ perum pakutiyip paṭip perukkattiṟkākac celaviṭṭu vantatu. cōḻap perunāṭṭil cōḻap pēraracar pala iṭaṅkaḷilum 74 paṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉar eṉiṉ, appaṭikaḷukkup perumporuḷ celavākiyirukka vēṇṭum allavā?
(2) aṟakkūḻccāli, vēṟu pala tāṉaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟkāka oru pakuti varuvāy celaviṭappaṭṭatu. kōvil kaṭṭutal,  pūci viḻākkaḷ ceytal, maṭaṅkaḷ, paḷḷikaḷ, maruttuva maṉikaḷ ivaṟṟi naṭatta utavutal, eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum aracāṅka varuvāyiṉ oru pakuti celaviṭappaṭṭatu.
(3) paṇṭit tamiḻaracar nallicip pulavarkaḷukkum, pāṇarkkum, kūttarkkum poruḷutavi ceytaṉar. uruttiraṉ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṟa pulavar, caṅkakālak karikāl vaḷavaṉmītu paṭṭiṉappāli pāṭip patiṉāyiram poṉ paricu peṟṟār (kaliṅkattupparaṇi, irācapārampariyam, kaṇṇi 21) iraṇṭām irācarācaṉ taṉmītu ulāp pāṭiya oṭṭakkūttarkku - oru kaṇṇikku āyiram poṉ vītam (391 kaṇṇikkum 391 āyiram poṉ) paricaḷittāṉ. (caṅkaracōḻaṉ ulā, kaṇṇi 25- 26.) ivvāṟu variyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracar palarāvar.
(4) āṟṟukku aṇi pōṭutal, kuḷam eṭuttal, kālvāy veṭṭutal, cattiram kaṭṭutal, pūṅkā amittal eṉṉum potuppaṇikaḷukku aracāṅka variyē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
(5) kaṭṭaṭakkali, ciṟpakkali, ōviyakkali, icikkali, vārppukkali mutaliyavaṟṟi ātarikkavum aracāṅka varuvāy celaviṭappaṭṭatu.
(6) aracāṅka varuvāyil kuṟippiṭat takka aḷavu araṇmaṉi alaṅkārattiṟkum, aracaṉuṭiya uṭi kuṭi antappura, kēḷikki ivaṟṟiṟkākavum celaviṭappaṭṭatu.
(7) āṇṭutōṟum varuvāyiṉ oru pakuti etirpārā nikaḻccikaḷukkāka otukki vikkappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉam āṇṭutōṟum otukki vikkappaṭum paṇam periya aḷavil aracāṅkap paṇṭārattil cērttu vikkappaṭṭatu. (S.I. polity, pp. 195- 196)

6. caṭṭam- muṟi- kāval
caṭṭaṅkaḷ
oru nāṭṭi āḷum aracāṅkam taṉatu āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa camutāyattiṉ nalvāḻvukkāka iyaṟṟum caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ tokuppē caṭṭam eṉṟu collappaṭum. paḻaṅkālac caṭṭaṅkaḷ perumpālum camayattiyum nītikaḷiyum aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru vakuppārum iṉṉa iṉṉavāṟu naṭantu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa vitikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. maṉitaṉ taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyilum camutāya muṟiyilum ivvulakil nalvāḻvu vāḻntu maṟuvulakil nalampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttuk koṇṭē, paḻaṅkālac caṭṭaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. (S.I. polity, p. 197)
aracāṅka taṇṭaṉi eṉpatu kuṭimakkaḷit tīneṟiyi ṉiṉṟum nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatākum. ātalāl, taṇṭaṉiyi aṟam eṉṟum aṟiñar kūṟuvar. aracāṅkam vitikkum caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷi mīṟuvōr taṇṭikkappaṭuvar eṉpatu ikkālattip pōlavē paḻaṅkālattilum naṉku vaṟpuṟuttap paṭṭatu. aracaṉ camutāyap pātukāppik kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. ataṟku avaṉ aṟatti aṭippaṭiyākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum; nītiyi aṉivarkkum vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum. 
	vēlaṉṟu veṉṟi taruvatu maṉṉavaṉ
	 kōlatūum kōṭāteṉiṉ
eṉpatu tamiḻ aṟam. aracaṉ koṭiyavarit taṇṭittal, payirik kākkak kaḷinīkkuvatu pōṉṟa ceyalākum eṉpatu vaḷḷuvar karuttu.
	koliyiṟ koṭiyāri vēntoṟuttal 				piṅkūḻ
	 kaḷikaṭ ṭataṉoṭu nēr
eṉavē, tīyōrit taṇṭittal aṟacceyalākavē karutappaṭṭatu. itu muṉṉarum kūṟappaṭṭatu.
aṟanūl
caṅka kālattil pacuviṉ maṭiyi aṟuttal, makaḷiratu karuvic citittal, pārppāri avamatittal mutaliyaṉa ceytavar periya aṟaṅkaḷic citittavar’ eṉṟu karutap paṭṭaṉar.(puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 34) oruvaṉ ceyta naṉmiyi maṟantavaṉukkō, naṉmikkup patilākat tīmi ceytavaṉukkō uyvilli eṉṟu aṟam pāṭiṟṟē āyiḻi kaṇava, eṉṟu pulavar ālantūr kiḻār cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉiṭam kūṟiṉār. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 34)  iṅku, aṟam eṉpatu nītinūl allatu aṟanūl.
    ennaṉṟi koṉṟāṟkum uyvuṇṭām uyvilli
 ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku
eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ. ālantūrkiḻār kuṟippiṭṭa aṟam ituvē. eṉavē, appulavar kālattil tirukkuṟaḷ aṟanūlākak karutappaṭṭatu pōlum!
akkālattil pōr toṭaṅka virumpiya aracar mutaṟkaṇ, yām num nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuppōm. num nāṭṭil uḷḷa pacukkaḷum, kuṟṟamaṟṟa pārppaṉarum, peṇṭirum, nōyuṭiyavarum, piḷḷi peṟātavarum ematu ampukaḷukku ilakkākāmal tappittuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ, eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapu. ittakiya pōrc caṭṭam akkālattil iruntu vantatu. itu aṟaneṟi eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce.9)
camutāyattukkuriya caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa nūlkaḷ aṟanūlkaḷ (taruma cāttiraṅkaḷ) eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa. avi vaṭamoḻiyil vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷukku eḻutap paṭṭaṉavē tavirat tamiḻ makkaḷukku alla. āyiṉum, caṅka kālattiṟkup piṉṉart tamiḻar allāta pallavar tamiḻakatti āḷat toṭaṅkiyatu mutal, vaṭavarkkāka eḻutappaṭṭa taruma cāttiraṅkaḷiṉpaṭi tamiḻakatti āḷat toṭaṅkiṉar. vaṭanāṭṭil mutalil taruma cāttiraṅkaḷiṉpaṭiyē caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. annūlkaḷukku uriyāciriyarkaḷ tōṉṟi viḷakkaṅkaḷ eḻutat toṭaṅkiya piṟaku, avvuriyāciriyarkaḷ palvēṟu iṉamakkaḷiṉ nīṇṭa kāla vaḻakkāṟukaḷiyum oppukkoṇṭaṉar. (S.I. polity. p. 198)
araca kuṭumpattiṉarik koli ceytal, aracaṉ utaviya aṟaṅkaḷic citittal, aracaṉ vititta taṇṭattic celuttāmi, kōvilukkeṉṟu aracaṉ āṇiyiṭṭa paṇattic celuttāmi, camutāya amitiyik keṭuttal pōṉṟavi aracat turōkac ceyalkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟē kirāmat turōkam, nāṭṭut turōkam eṉṟum, ittakiya tavaṟukaḷ iṭattukku ēṟpap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ūravikaḷ, kirāmat turōkattukkut taṇṭaṉi vitittaṉa. avvāṟē nāṭāḷvār nāṭṭut turōkikaḷit taṇṭittaṉar.


vēṭṭic caṭṭam mutaliyaṉa
uṭukkiyi otta kālkaḷi uṭiyaṉavum, maṭinta kātukaḷi uṭiyaṉavum ākiya yāṉik kaṉṟukaḷ mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kuṭṭikaḷiyum cūl koṇṭa peṇ vilaṅku kaḷiyum vēṭarkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭār eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār purāṇattil kūṟiyuḷḷār. (ce.86) ikkālattil vilaṅkukaḷip pātukākkum ittakiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. cōḻar amiccarāṉa cēkkiḻār itaṉik kūṟiyiruttalāl, cōḻar kālattil ittakiya caṭṭam oṉṟu iruntirukkalām eṉṟu karutuvatu poruttamākum.
koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷāl tamakkum tam uṭimikaṭkum tīṅku nēruvatāka vēṭar, kaṇṇappariṉ tantiyāṉa vēṭṭuva aracaṉiṭam muṟiyiṭṭaṉar. (ce. 43) avaṉ kuṭikaḷiṉ uyirukkum uṭimikkum tīṅku varāmaṟkāppatu taṉ kaṭami ātalāl, koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷi aḻikkat tuṇintāṉ. uṭalippaṟṟiya kuṟṟam (offences aginst person),  uṭamiyip paṟṟiya kuṟṟam (Offences aginst property) eṉpavi nērāmaṟ kāppatu araciṉ kaṭami eṉṟu iṉṟum caṭṭam kūṟukiṟataṉṟō? (C.K.S. mutaliyār, cēkkiḻār, pak 50)  itu muṟkālattum vaḻakkil iruntatu eṉpatu itaṉāl terikiṟataṉṟō?
oruvar aṭimāṉamāka vitta uṭimi paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ variyilumē aṭimāṉam peṟṟavariṭam iruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṟaku uriyavarkaḷiṭam evvitat tokiyum kēṭkāmal uṭimiyi uṭiyavariṭam oppuvittal vēṇṭum eṉpatu vijayanakara āṭciyil caṭṭamāka iruntatu.  (619 of 1917)
oruvaratu aracāṅkap paṇikkāka vaḻaṅkappaṭum iṉām nilaṅkaḷ aṭimāṉam vikkavō, viṟkavō kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉam ceypavar, aracatturōkikaḷum camutāyatturōki kaḷum peṟattakum taṇṭaṉiyip peṟuvar eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (193 of 1916)
ki.pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ōr ūric cērnta pirāmaṇarkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkūṭi, kaṉṉikātāṉamākat tammuḷ maṇam muṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟum, itaṟku māṟāka naṭappavar cātinīkkam ceyyappaṭuvatōṭu aracaṉāl taṇṭikkappaṭuvar eṉṟum tīrmāṉittaṉar eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (428 of 1887) iṅṅaṉam ōr iṉattār muṭivu caṭṭamākiṟatu eṉpatu aṟiyattakum.
nīti maṉṟaṅkaḷ
aracaṉ kuṭikaḷukkut tantiyum talivaṉum āvāṉ. eṉavē, kuṭikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi vicārittu muṟi vaḻaṅkutal aracaṉatu ciṟappuk kaṭamiyākiṟatu. caṅka kālattil vaḷḷuvar naṭuvunilimi eṉṉum atikārattāl nīti maṉṟattār kaṭamikaḷi uṇarttiyuḷḷār; ulakattu uyirkaḷ maḻiyi nōkki vāḻum; atupōlak kuṭikaḷ maṉṉaṉatu neṟi tavaṟā muṟiyi nōkki vāḻvar. kuṭimakkaḷuṭiya kuṟiyip poṟumiyōṭu kēṭṭu muṟi vaḻaṅkāta aracaṉ taṉṉittāṉē aḻittukkoḷvāṉ eṉpatu vaḷḷuvar vākku. (atikāram 55, 2, 8)
      camaṉceytu cīrtūkkum kōlpōl amintorupāl
   kōṭāmi cāṉṟōrk kaṇi. (atikāram 12, 8.)
eṉpatum apperumāṉ vākkākum.
cōḻanāṭṭut talinakarāṉa uṟiyūr nītimaṉṟattiṟkup peyar pōṉatu eṉṟu caṅkappāṭal kuṟikkiṉṟatu. (maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟanti aviyattu;aṟaniṉṟu niliyiṟ ṟākaliṉ ataṉāl; muṟiminiṉ pukaḻum aṉṟē. -puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 39) paṇṭikkālattil araṇmaṉi vācalil ārāyccimaṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. nīti vēṇṭuvōr ammaṇiyi acittu ōci uṇṭākkuvar. uṭaṉē maṉṉaṉ avarkaḷit taṉ kolumaṇṭapattiṟku aḻittu varacceytu avartam kuṟikaḷik kēṭpāṉ; ēṟṟamuṟiyil nīti vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. maṉunīticcōḻaṉ varalāṟē paṇṭit tamiḻaracartam nīti muṟikku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum. (periyapurāṇam, tirunakarac ciṟappu, ce. 27- 44.)
kaṇṇakiyiṉ vaḻakkip pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, taṉ araṇmaṉiyil mātaviyōṭu taṉittiruntapōtu, mikavum poṟumiyākak kēṭṭāṉ eṉpatiliruntu, avacarakālaṅ kaḷil aracaṉ enta iṭattiliruntum vaḻakkukaḷi vicārippatu vaḻakkam eṉpatu terikiṟatu, iḷiñaṉāka irukkum tāṉ muṟi tavaṟātu nīti vaḻaṅkuvāṉō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa iruvarkku, mutiya nītipati vēṭattil tōṉṟik karikālaṉ muṟi vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟu paḻam pāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu. (paḻamoḻi, ce.6.)
caṅkakāla - nītimaṉṟam maṉṟam, avi, avikkaḷam, aṟakkaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. nītipatikaḷ aṟakkaḷattu antaṇar eṉap paṭṭaṉar eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. (kāti 22, vari 8.)
pallavar kālattil uyarnīti maṉṟam tarmācaṉam eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. kirāmaṅkaḷil ūr avikaḷ iruntaṉa. ūr aviyārē ūr vaḻakkukaḷit tīrttaṉar; uriya taṇṭaṉikaḷi vitittaṉar. yāṉum eṉ marapiṉarum ivarukku aṭimi eṉṟu cuntarar pāṭṭaṉār eḻutik koṭut tiruppatāl, cuntarar tamakku aṭimi eṉṟu vātiṭṭa kiḻa vētiyar vaḻakkit tiruveṇṇey nallūrc capiyiṉarē naṭuvu niliyiliruntu ellāvaṟṟiyum vicārittut tīrttaṉar. ivvāṟē ciṟuvarāṉa vicāra carumar ceyta tavaṟṟukku avar tanti vicārikkappaṭṭāṉ, avaṉ aviyōriṭam maṉṉippu vēṇṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu periya purāṇam pēcukiṉṟatu. (taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa purāṇam, ce. 469; caṇṭēcuravar purāṇam, ce.40 - 43.)
cōḻar āṭciyil ittakiya ūr avikaḷum capikaḷum nāṭukaḷum (nāṭṭu maṉṟaṅkaḷum) vaḻakkukaḷi vicārittu nalla muṟiyil muṭivu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatip pala kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟikiṟōm.
vicayanakara āṭcik kālattil piratāṉi (mutalamiccaṉ) nītimaṉṟa uyar atikāriyāka iruntāṉ. cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉum piratāṉiyum amarntu vaḻakkit tīrppatuṇṭu. maturi vīrappa nāyakkar āṭciyil ovvoru kirāmattilum nītipatikaḷ iruvar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ ūr makkaḷāl mikavum matikkappaṭṭaṉar. (maturi nāyakkar, pak. 241.)
oru nāṭṭic cērnta ovvoru mākāṇattilum mākāṇa āḷunar (kavarṉar) allatu aracap piratiniti mākāṇa nītimaṉṟattil talimi tāṅki muṟi ceytu vantaṉar. ūrāṭci maṉṟattār, kōvil atikārikaḷ, caṅkattār, cātit talivarkaḷ eṉpavar ūr vaḻakkukaḷit tīrttaṉar. ittakiya ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷ muṭivu ceyyum vaḻakkukaḷil cila, aracaṉit talivaṉākak koṇṭa uyarnīti maṉṟattiṟkuc celvatum uṇṭu. (S.I. Polity,p. 208)
nīti naṭimuṟi
tirunāvukkaracar camaṇattik kaṇṭittup piracāram ceyta kāraṇattāl avari vicārittut taṇṭikka virumpiya pallava makēntiravarmaṉ amiccariyum paṭiyiyum aṉuppit tirunāvukkaracic ciṟi ceytāṉ. (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, ce. 90-91.) celvākkuḷḷa periyavarip periya atikārikaḷ ceṉṟu mariyātiyuṭaṉ ciṟi ceytal iṉṟum kāṇalām.
tirunāvukkaracar civaliṅkattit taricikkaviṭāmaṟ paḻiyāṟiyil camaṇar ceyta kuḻcciyi aracaṉ aṟintu vicārittuc camaṇarit taṇṭittāṉ; (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, ce. 297- 298.)  taṇṭiyaṭikaḷip paḻittup pēciya camaṇarkkum taṇṭiyaṭikaṭkum uṇṭāṉa vivātatti vicārittu, nēril uṇmi uṇarntu nīti vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. (taṇṭiyaṭikaḷ purāṇam, ce. 15- 20.)  camayattip pātukāppatu maṉṉar kaṭami eṉpati akkāla aracarum uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu ivaṟṟāl aṟiyalām.
cuntararuṭiya tantikkut tantiyār veṇṇey nallūrp pittaṉārkkut tāmum tam vaḻiyiṉarum aṭimi eṉṟu icivu (oppantam) eḻutikkoṭuttiruntār. atit tiruveṇṇey nallūrppiccaṉ koṇṭuvantu cuntarar tamakku aṭimi eṉṟu coṉṉār. appoḻutu avvūrc capiyiṉar paṭittup pārttaṉar; āṭci, āvaṇam, ayalār kāṭci eṉpavaṟṟi ārāyntaṉar; ōliyil irunta kiyeḻuttit tam araṇtaru kāppil irunta mūla ōlikkiyeḻuttōṭu oppiṭṭaṉar; piṉṉarē icivu vaḻakkil muṭivu kūṟiṉar. icceyti taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa purāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. (taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa purāṇam, ce. 59- 63.)
oru vaḻakkil nītittīrppukkut tuṇi ceyvaṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭṭa cāṭciyaṅkaḷākum. avi āṭci, āvaṇam, ayalār kāṭci eṉpaṉa. āṭci eṉpatu, toṉṟutoṭṭu, atāvatu ittaṉi kālam eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭāta kālamāyc, cāṉṟōrāṟ kiyāḷap peṟṟu, aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭa oḻukkamām. ataṉiyē āṉṟōr āṭci eṉpar. āvaṇam eṉpatu, oru vaḻakkit tīrmāṉikka utavum cuvaṭi ōli mutaliya eḻuttuc cīṭṭukkaḷ. ayalār kāṭci eṉpatu vaḻakku nikaḻcciyik kaṇṭār celvatu. ivi muṟiyē (Oral Evidence) vāymoḻik kūṟu eṉṟum, (Documentary Evidence) cuvaṭik kūṟu eṉṟum taṟkāla āṅkila nīti muṟiyil pēcappeṟum. ayalār taṅkaḷ kāṭci eṉpataṉāl tāmē kaṇṭatu cāṉṟākumaṉṟip piṟar kaṇṭatikkēṭṭup pēcuvatu cāṉṟākātu eṉṟu otukkappeṟṟatum teriyalām. Hearsay is not evidence eṉṟa innāḷ vitiyum kāṇka. āvaṇam eṉpavi annāḷil ōlikaḷil eḻutap peṟṟaṉa. avikaḷil eḻutikkoṭuttōr peyarum, eḻuti vāṅkikkoṇṭōr peyarum, eḻutum poruḷiṉ vivaramum aṭaṅkum. iṟutiyil itaṟku ivi eṉ eḻuttu eṉṟa eḻutikkoṭuttōr kiyeḻuttum iṭappaṭṭirukkum. oppantaṅkaḷukku icivu eṉpatu peyar. ivvaki oppantaṅkaḷ paṇṭi vaḻakkattiṟkum āṭcikkum māṟutalākavum irukkalām. avvāṟu ulaka oḻukkattiṟkum māṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, icivu capiyārukkut teḷivu peṟuttappeṟṟāl, atuvē tīrmāṉikkappaṭum. āṭcikku virōtameṉṟu oru icivu vaḻakkit taḷḷiviṭa māṭṭārkaḷ. icivu vaḻakku eṉpatu iruvar oppantat tiṉippaṟṟik kōrum tīrmāṉatti uṭkoṇṭa tām. avvaḻakkukaḷit tāṉ cuses based on agreement eṉṟu āṅkila muṟi collum. avvāṟu eḻutik koṭukkum icivu ōlikaḷukkuc cāṭcikkārarkaḷ kiyiḻuttup pōṭuvatum vaḻakkam. cāṭcik kiyeḻuttup pōṭupavarkku mēleḻuttiṭuvōr eṉṟu peyarām. eḻutik koṭuttavar iṟantu paṭṭāl, cāṭcikkārarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttiyum eḻutikkoṭuttavariṉ kiyoppattiyum vēṟu cācaṉaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu, avi aviyē eṉṟu niccayittut tīrppukkoṭuppārkaḷ. avvāṟu iṟantōr kiyeḻuttukkaḷi avarkaḷuṭiyaṉavām eṉṟa niccayikkiṟataṟku, avarkaḷuṭiyaṉa eṉa niccayamāy aṟinta vēṟu kiyeḻuttu ōlikaḷi varavaḻittu, iraṇṭu kiyeḻuttukkaḷiyum oppunōkki niccayippatum tīrmāṉippatum annāḷ vaḻakku vicārikkum muṟikaḷil oṉṟākum. itaṉāl Comparison of handwriting and signatures eṉṟu innāḷ āṅkila nīti muṟikkārar koṇṭuḷḷa vitikaḷ, pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ akkālattē tamiḻarkaḷ kiyāṇṭa vitikaḷāka nām aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu kiyeḻuttukkaḷ ottup pārppataṟkāka anta ūrkaḷil uḷḷavarkaḷatu kiyeḻuttukkaḷiyum kiyoppaṅkaḷiyum taṉiyāka ūrppotu aracāṅka nīticcālip pātukāppil kāppāṟṟi vikkum vaḻakkamum iruntatu. itu innāḷ pattirap pativu (Registration) iyalār ceyyum muṟiyiviṭa mēṟpaṭṭa muṟiyil viḷaṅkuvatu kāṇalām. im muṟikaḷ taṭuttāṭ koṇṭa purāṇattilē cuntaramūrttikaḷip paramacivaṉār valvaḻakkiṭṭu veṇṇey nallūrc capiyilē tamatu āḷ ōliyik kāṭṭit tam pakkam tīrppuppeṟṟu āṭkoṇṭaruḷiya varalāṟukaḷilum piṟavaṟṟālum naṉku aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ūrkaḷtōṟum uḷḷa nītimuṟic capikaḷilē takkōr iruntu cenneṟiyilē namar- piṟar eṉṟa evvakip pētamum, orupāṟ kōṭip pārapaṭcam ceytalum illāmal, iruvakiyum oppanāṭi nītit tīrppuc ceytu vantārkaḷ. antac capikaḷukkuk karaṇattāṉ (kumātā- clerk) oruvaṉ uṇṭu. avaṉē antac capiyār ēvaliṉpaṭi nīti vicāraṇik kāriyaṅkaḷil utavi ceyvāṉ. avaṉē nīti maṉṟattil cuvaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟik kāval puripavaṉ. (C.K.S. mutaliyār, cōḻar, pak. 66- 68.)
māṟavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyatēvaṉ kālattil kuḷattūr makkaḷum, avvūrk kōvil atikārikaḷum oru kaṭciyāka iruntaṉar; vikkiramacōḻa nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉpavaṉ etirkkaṭciyāḷaṉ. āṟṟu nīr urimi paṟṟi ivvirutiṟat tārukkum pūcal uṇṭāṉatu. irutiṟattāriyum cāṉṟōr vicārittu, āṟṟil oru kuṟippiṭṭa pakutiyil taṇṇīr eṭukkalām eṉṟum, mīṉ piṭikkum variyil oru pāti kōvilukkum maṟupāti vikkiramacōḻa nāṭāḷvāṉukkum cēravēṇṭum eṉṟum muṭivu ceytaṉar. (380 of 1914)
tiruvatiki tiruvīraṭṭāṉēcuvarar kōvilic cērnta tāṉattār, mākēcuvarar ākiyōrukkum atirāja maṅkaliya purattu nakarattārkkum elli paṟṟiya vaḻakku oṉṟu naṭantatu. vīrapāṇṭiyatēvaṉ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭa muṭivi nakarattār oppukkoḷḷavilli. iraṇṭāvatākavum mūṉṟāvatākavum ceyyappaṭṭa muṭivukaḷi mākēcuvararum tāṉattārum oppukkoḷḷavilli. eṉavē, aracāṅka atikārikaḷāṉa uṭiyār kāliṅkarāyar, naraciṅkatēva mutaliyār, tirumañcaṉa māḷikiyār, vicuvanātatēvar eṉpavarkaḷ avvaḻakki vicārittu muṭivu ceytaṉar eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (380 of 1914; 69 of 1924; S.I. polity, p. 214.)
vaḻakki vicārittu muṭivu kūṟuvōr iṭattiṟkēṟpavum vaḻakkiṟku ēṟpavum vēṟupaṭṭiruntaṉar. uṭiyārkuṭik kōvil vaḻakkukaḷit tīrkka akkōvil mākēcuvarar, pala kōvilkaḷic cērnta atikārikaḷ, nāṭṭiṉ pala pakutikaḷic cērnta yātrikar, uṭiyārkuṭiyil irunta kikkōḷa mutalikaḷ mutaliyōr naṭuvarāka iruntaṉar eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu.  (570 of 1920; A.R.E. 1921, para 40)
ittakiya vaḻakku muṭipukaḷil amiti peṟātavar, maṇṭalat talivaṉukkō allatu aracaṉukkō viṇṇappam ceytukoḷḷalām. avarkaḷ tāmākavē muṭivukūṟa muṭiyātapōtu, nāṭṭār utaviyi nāṭuvar. avarkaḷālum muṭiyātapōtu, cuṟṟuppuṟa ūrkaḷic cērnta makācaṉaṅkaḷ vaḻakki vicārittu muṭivu ceyvārkaḷ. vicaya nakara āṭcik kālattil cēlam māvaṭṭattic cērnta ārakaḷūr civaṉkōvil vaḻakku oṉṟu mēṟcollappaṭṭa muṟiyil tīrttu vikkappaṭṭatu. (413 of 1913; A.R.E. 1914, para 26.)
nilaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya cila vaḻakkukaḷil, nītipatikaḷ nilaṅkaḷip pārtta piṟakē muṭivukūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. avaravarkkuriya pattiraṅkaḷiyum nilaṅkaḷiyum pari cōtitta piṟakē vaḻakku oṉṟu tīrkkappaṭṭatu. maturi nāyakkar kālattil srīvilliputtūril (patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil) ittakiya vaḻakku oṉṟu tīrkkappaṭṭatu. (582 of 1926)
cila vaḻakkukaḷil pōtumāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ iruppatilli. vaḻakkāḷikaḷ iruvarumē oruvariyoruvar kuṟi kūṟuvar. anta niliyil nītipatikaḷ tām aiyappaṭupavaric cila cōtaṉikaḷukku uḷḷākkuvar. kāycciya irumpik kiyil piṭittal, tī mitittal, kotikkum neyyil kiyi viṭutal mutaliyaṉa akkālac cōtaṉikaḷākum. iccōtaṉi kaḷukkuṭpaṭṭa vaḻakkāḷikaḷ oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuḷ accōtaṉiyāl tuṉpuṟātiruntāl, avarkaḷ vaḻakkil veṉṟavarkaḷāvar; tuṉpuṟṟāl vaḻakkil tōṟṟavarākak karutappaṭuvar. oru vaḻakkiṉ muṭivu, vaḻakkāṭiya iruvaruḷ oruvarukkut tirupti illi eṉṟāl, avar veṉṟavaric cōtaṉikku uḷḷākkumpaṭi atikārikaḷi vēṇṭik koḷḷalām. ki.pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ittakiya vaḻakku oṉṟu naṭantatu eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (S.I. polity, pp. 223- 224)
kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷ
koli, kaḷavu, cōram, māṟṟuk kiyeḻuttiṭutal mutaliyavi kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷic (Criminal) cērntavi. ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷic ceytavar aracāṅka vēlikkut takutiyaṟṟavar eṉpatu tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭatu. (A.R.E. 1899, para 63.) ēṉiya vaḻakkukaḷip pōlavē kaṭun taṇṭaṉikkuriya vaḻakkukaḷum ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷilum, nāṭṭu maṉṟaṅkaḷilum vicārittu muṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa; tēvi ēṟpaṭiṉ, aracaṉum amiccaṉum nēril vicārittu nīti vaḻaṅkiṉar.
oruvaṉ vēṭṭiyāṭumpōtu kuṟitavaṟi oru veḷḷāḷaṉik koṉṟu viṭṭāṉ. avaṉi vicārikka eḻupattoṉpatu nāṭukaḷic cērnta talivarkaḷ kūṭiṉar; taṉ kuṟṟattiṟku īṭāka avaṉatu ūrkkōvilil iraṇṭu nantā viḷakkukaḷ erippataṟkāka aṟupattu nāṉku āṭukaḷik kōvilukku viṭumpaṭi tīrppuk kūṟiṉar. (67 of 1906)
oru nāṭāḷvāṉ viṟpaṭiyiṉariṉ talivaruḷ oruvaṉik kuttik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. iraṇṭām irācēntira cōḻaṉ, akkuṟṟavāḷi kōvilil oru nantā viḷakku vikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kirāma atikārikaḷukku āṇi pōkkiṉāṉ. (227 of 1904)
oru veḷḷāḷaṉ, tavaṟutalāka vēṟoruvaṉik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. aracāṅka atikāri avvaḻakki vicārittāṉ. kōvil paṭṭarkaḷ, avaṉ vēḷāḷaṉāka iruttalāl kolit taṇṭaṉi kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. aracāṅka atikāri anta muṭivi ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. (200 of 1929;   oru kulattukku oru nīti iruntamiyik kāṇka.)
oru vaṇikaṉ vippāṭṭi vittiruntāṉ. maṟṟoruvaṉ ōr iravu avaḷi keṭukka muṉintāṉ. vaṇikaṉ aṅṅaṉam vantavaṉik kuttik koṉṟāṉ. ivvaḻakki vicāritta periyavarkaḷ, vaṇikaṉum, iṟantavaṉatu neruṅkiṉa uṟaviṉaṉum āka, iruvarum cērntu uḷḷūrc civaṉ kōvilil oru nantā viḷakku erikkaṭṭum eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟiṉar. (77 of 1906)
vēṇṭumeṉṟē naṭipeṟṟa koṭiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭun taṇṭaṉikaḷ vitikkappaṭṭaṉa.
aintu pirāmaṇa cakōtararkaḷum cila veḷḷāḷarkaḷum taṅkaḷ cātit toḻilkaḷi viṭṭuk kīḻ vakuppār toḻili mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ koṭiya karuvikaḷik koṇṭu pirāmaṇarkaḷik koli ceytaṉar; pirāmaṇap peṇkaḷi iḻivu paṭuttiṉar; koḷḷi aṭittaṉar; kāl naṭikaḷik kippaṟṟi viṟṟaṉar. ikkoṭumikaḷi vikkirama cōḻaṉ atikārikaḷukku (itu naṭanta nāṭṭu atikārikaḷukku)t terivikkappaṭṭatu. atikārikaḷ akkuṟṟavāḷikaḷip piṭittu aṭittaṉar; taṇṭam vitittaṉar; avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷi aḻittaṉar. ittaṇṭaṉikaḷāl avarkaḷ tiruntavilli. uṭaṉē makkaḷ anta nāṭṭi āṇṭa pottappi ariyar eṉpavariṭam muṟiyiṭṭaṉar. avar oru paṭiyi ēviṉār. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ appaṭi vīraruḷ cilarik koṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷuṭiya pōrk karuvikaḷiyum kikkoṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, paṭivīrar, kuṟṟampurinta pirāmaṇa cakōtarar aivaruḷ iruvaric ciṟippiṭittu, aracaṉ muṉṉiliyil koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. avvamayam maṟṟa mūṉṟu cakōtararkaḷum kāvalarik koṉṟu kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi viṭuvittaṉar. itaṉi aṟinta aracaṉ, kīḻvakuppiṉarit taṇṭittaṟku uriya vitikaḷiṉpaṭi akkuṟṟavāḷikaḷit taṇṭikkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭāṉ; attuṭaṉ, avarkaḷatu vaḻivaḻic cottu kōvilukkum aṟaniliyaṅkaḷukkum viṟkappaṭavēṇṭum, viṟṟuvarum tokiyiliruntu avarkaḷ ceyta kuṟṟaṅkaḷuk kuriya taṇṭaṉit tokiyi eṭuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum, eñciya tokiyi akkuṟṟavāḷikaḷ peyaril kōvilukkut tāṉamāka aḷittuviṭa vēṇṭum, eṉṟu āṇi piṟappittāṉ. (315 of 1909; A.R. E. 1910, para 34.)
inta nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu nām aṟivaṉa yāvi? ūr aviyār kuṟṟavāḷikaḷip piṭikka iyalātapōtu, aracāṅka atikārikaḷum aracaṉum taliyiṭṭu vaḻakki muṭivu ceyvar eṉpatu terikiṟatu; mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ potu makkaḷāl piṭikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatum, avarkaḷ kīḻ vakuppiṉarkkuriya taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatum terikiṉṟaṉa.
atikāri muṉṉiliyil kuṟṟavāḷikaḷē tammuḷ oru muṭivukku varutalum uṇṭu. putukkōṭṭic cīmiyic cērnta teṟkup pakuti makkaḷukkum vaṭakkup pakuti makkaḷukkum māṟupāṭu uṇṭāṉatu; ataṉāl pala kolikaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. vaṭapakutiyiṉar mikka aṭṭūḻiyam ceytaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ makkaḷil cilarit teṟkup pakutiyiṉariṭam oppaṭittaṉar; kōvil atikārikaḷ muṉṉiliyil ōr oppantam ceytu koṇṭaṉar. ataṉpaṭi irutiṟattārum oruvarukkoruvar tīṅkiḻikkalākātu. itaṟku māṟāka naṭantāl irutiṟattārum kūṭittīmi ceypavaṉ nilaṅkaḷik kippaṟṟik kōvilukkut tāṉamāka viṭalām, aṅṅaṉam tāṉamāka viṭappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷukku antak kuṟṟavāḷiyē vari celutta vēṇṭum, ivaṟṟōṭu ūravikkum aracaṉukkum avaṉ taṇṭam celuttavum nēralām eṉṟu oppantam ceytaṉar. (Ins. of. pudukkotti State 683)
aracat turōkam ceytavar cottu aracāṅkattārāl kippaṟṟappaṭṭatu. atturōkikaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil kollappaṭṭaṉar; avarkaḷ nilaṅkaḷ ēlam viṭappaṭṭaṉa. (112 of 1911; 246 of 1917, 506 of 1918, 279 of 1927)
cila camayaṅkaḷil kuṟṟavāḷi kaṭavuḷ cilikku muṉ uṟuti moḻi vāṅkappaṭuvāṉ; tāṉ kuṟṟam ceyyavilli eṉṟu kaṭavuḷ vikkirattukku muṉ cattiyam ceyvāṉ; neruppil kāynta irumput tuṇṭi nāvāl nakkuvāṉ. nāvil kāyam ēṟpaṭāviṭiṉ, avaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟavaṉākak karutappaṭuvāṉ; kuṟṟavāḷi cila camayaṅkaḷil teyvac cilikku muṉ cattiyam ceytuviṭṭu, neruppil kāyntu koṇṭirukkum irumpi eṭuppatumuṇṭu. kāyam paṭṭāl kuṟṟattukku uriya taṇṭam vitikkappaṭuvāṉ; cilikkumuṉ oru pāttirattil kāycciya ney irukkum. tāṉ kuṟṟam ceyyavilli eṉṟu cattiyam ceytavaṉ, iraṇṭu viralkaḷi neyyuḷ viṭuvāṉ. uṭaṉē avviralkaḷ oru tuṇiyāl cuṟṟappaṭṭu muttiri vikkappaṭum. mūṉṟām nāḷ akkaṭṭu nīkkappaṭum. viralkaḷil kāyam iruntāl avaṉ kaṭumiyāka taṇṭikkappaṭuvāṉ. immūṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷum ki.pi. 15- ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vicaya nakara āṭciyil vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. (S.I. polity, p. 233.)
kuṭumiyāṉ malik kōvil karuvaṟiyiliruntu vili yuyarnta naki kaḷavāṭappaṭṭatu. aiyuṟappaṭṭa civapirāmaṇarip pakkattuc ciṟṟaracarkaḷ - pērūrkaḷ - nāṭukaḷ ivaṟṟic cērnta talivarkaḷ kūṭi vicārittaṉar; periya paṭṭarkaḷiyum kalantu yōcittaṉar. ammaṇṭalat talivaṉ muṉṉiliyil ivvicāraṇi naṭipeṟṟatu. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ kāycciya irumpip piṭikkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉar. nālvar kikaḷil kāyam paṭṭatu. maṟṟoru kuṟṟavāḷi taṉ kuṟṟatti oppukkoṇṭāṉ. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ aṉivarum civat turōkikaḷ eṉṟu tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭuc ciṟiyil aṭikkap paṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya nilaṅkaḷum piṟa uṭimikaḷum tirunāmattukkāṇi eṉṟa peyaril kōvilukkuc cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. accottukkaḷmītu muṉpē paṇam koṭuttavar, kōviliruntu paṇam peṟṟuk koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ aṭimāṉatti viṭumpaṭik kaṭṭaḷi piṟappikkappaṭṭatu. (Ins. of pudukkotti; State, 691.)
cila kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷiliruntu kōvil atikārikaḷum ūr aviyiṉarum tiruṭu mutaliya kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷi vicārikkum urimi peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. oru kōvil tiruṭṭu vaḻakkil kuṟṟavāḷiyiṉ cottup paṟimutal ceyyappaṭṭatu; oru ki veṭṭap paṭṭatu; avaṉ kirāmattiliruntu viraṭṭappaṭṭāṉ. (Ins. of pudukkotti; State 867.)
taṇṭaṉi
kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷil kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu taṇṭaṉi meṉmiyākavum vaṉmiyākavum iruntu vantatu. paṇam taṇṭamāka vitittal, ciṟiyil kikālkaḷi veṭṭutal, tūkkiliṭutal, kuttik koli ceytal, yāṉiyiṉ kālāl iṭaṟuvittal mutaliya taṇṭaṉikaḷ koṭiya kuṟṟavāḷi kaḷukku vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟōṭu kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ cottukkaḷ paṟimutal ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
caṅka kālattil cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ maliyamāṉ makkaḷi yāṉikku iṭṭuk kolla niṉittāṉ eṉpatu puṟappāṭalāl aṟikiṉṟōm. (ce. 46.)
pallavar kālattil camaṇa camayattit tākkip piracāram ceyta tirunāvukkaracar yāṉiyāl kolla viṭappaṭṭār; viṭam kalanta uṇavu uṇṇumāṟu tūṇṭappaṭṭār; nīṟṟaṟiyil pōṭappaṭṭār; kallōṭu kaṭṭik kaṭalil eṟipaṭṭār. (tirunāvukkaracar purāṇam, ce. 95- 132.) ivi pallavar kālattuk koṭuntaṇṭaṉikaḷ eṉṉalām.
piṟkālac cōḻar āṭciyil, kuṟṟam vaṉmi vāyntatāyiṉ, kuṟṟavāḷi maraccaṭṭattil kaṭṭappaṭuvāṉ; 50 mutal 100 aṭi vari taṭikoṇṭu aṭikkappaṭuvāṉ. koṭiya kuṟṟamāyiṉ, yāṉiyiṉ kālāl iṭaṟappaṭṭuk kollap paṭuvāṉ, eṉṟu cu-ju- kuvā eṉṟa cīṉa yāttirikar kuṟittuḷḷār. (F.N. of S. India, p. 143)
vicaya nakara āṭciyil, tiruṭaṉuṭiya oru kāliyum kiyiyum veṭṭiviṭṭaṉar. mikap periya aḷavil tiruṭṭu naṭattiya kuṟṟavāḷi tūkkiliṭappaṭṭāṉ. aracat turōkam ceyta perumakkaḷ (vayiṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka) kaḻuvēṟṟappaṭṭaṉar; kūrmiyāṉa marakkaḻu celuttappaṭṭuk kollappaṭṭaṉar. aracat turōkam ceyta cātāraṇa makkaḷ potu iṭaṅkaḷil niṟuttappaṭṭuk kaḻuttu veṭṭappaṭṭaṉar. aracaṉiyē kolla niṉitta koṭiyavar neruppil iṭṭuk kollappaṭṭaṉar. avar tam kuṭumpaṅkaḷ nāṭṭiviṭṭē viraṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil iṟakkum niliyil kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ cittiravati ceyyappaṭṭaṉar.  (Sewell, A Forgotten Empire, p. 383- 4.)
caṅka kālattil kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi vikkac ciṟic cālikaḷ iruntaṉa. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil maṇimēkali yiṉ kālattil ciṟikkōṭṭam iruntatu. atu avaḷ vēṇṭukōḷāl aṟakkōṭṭam ākkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu maṇimēkali kāppiyam kūṟukiṟatu. piṟkālattilum ciṟikaḷ iruntaṉa.
oruvaṉik koṉṟavaṉ kollappaṭṭavaṉuṭiya ūrkkōvilil viḷakkerippatāka oppuk koḷkiṟāṉ. itaṟkuk kollappaṭṭavaṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷ iciyiṉ, koliyāḷi taṇṭaṉiyiṉṟi viṭappaṭuvāṉ. itaṉik kuṟikkum kalveṭṭukkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. (48 of 1897 etc.)
cila pakutikaḷil kaṭumiyāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkut taṇṭap paṇam vacūlikkappaṭum. oru kaṉṉip peṇṇik keṭuttavaṉ 108 poṉ taṇṭam kaṭṭavēṇṭum; vēṟu peṇ piḷḷiyik keṭuttavaṉ 32 poṉ taṇṭam celutta vēṇṭum; kāti aṟuttavaṉ 16 paṇam kaṭṭavēṇṭum eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. (E.I. 25.P 238.) ic ceytikaḷi uṇarttum kalveṭṭukkaḷ cilavuṇṭu. (Ins. of pudukkotti State, 484, 913.)
kāvaṟ paṭi
ūrkaḷil kaḷavu mutaliyaṉa naṭipeṟātu kāppatu ūrkkāvalar kaṭami. ūrkkāvalarē ikkālattil pōlīcār eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maturiyil caṅka kālattil ūrkkāvalar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ, aṉivarum kaṇṇuṟaṅkum naṭu yāmattil nakarat terukkaḷil cuṟṟi varuvar; puli pōlum vīramuṭiyavar; añcāmi mikkavar; kaḷvariṉ cūḻccikaḷi aṟintavar. kuṟi tavaṟātu ampu eyvatil vallavar; maḻiyāl teruvil nīr mikuntiruppiṉum, iruḷ kavintu iruppiṉum, tam kaṭamiyic ceyvar. avarkaḷ kariya niṟattiṉar; kariya uṭiyiṉar. avarkaḷ kiyil vāḷ piṭittiruppar. avarkaḷ iṭiyil nūlēṇi irukkum; kāṟṟiṉum kaṭitiṟ cellum kaḷvariyum kaṭṭip piṭikkum valimi uṭiyavar eṉṟu maturik kāñci terivikkiṉṟatu. (maturikkāñci, vari 632- 647) cōḻa nāṭṭu nakaraṅkaḷil iravil iṭiyāmattil ūr kāppavar viḷakkuṭaṉ ceṉṟu ūrik kāttaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 37.)
ciṟṟūrkaḷil ūrkkāvalar pōlī vēliyic ceytu vantaṉar. ivvāṟē nāṭu eṉṉum perum pirivi nāṭṭukkāvalar kāttu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ muṟiyē pāṭi kāvalar eṉṟum nāṭu kāvalar eṉṟum kalveṭṭukkaḷil peyar peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukku ūric cērnta ovvoru vīṭṭārum āṇṭukku ivvaḷavu nel eṉṟu koṭuttu vantaṉar. pala ūrkaḷil ūraviyiṉar ivarkaḷukkeṉṟu nilattic contamāka viṭṭiruntaṉar. ūrkkāval allatu nāṭu kāval muṟiyē parampari vēliyāka iruntu vantatu. akkāvalar aracat turōkam ceyyiṉ, vēli pōyviṭum; nilamum paṟikkappaṭum. ūrār kāvalukkuk koṭuppatu nel maṭṭumaṉṟu. parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil nel viḷiyum ovvoru mā nilattilum oru kalam nellum, ovvoru pākku marattiṟkum vīcam paṇamum, karumpu, marukkoḻuntu, iñci, vāḻi viḷiyum ovvoru mā nilattiṟkum 5 paṇamum ovvoru vīṭṭiṟkum 2 paṇamum ūrkkāvalarukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. (407 of 1921)
ūriṉ aḷavukkut takkapaṭi ūrkkāvalar cilarāyum, palarāyum iruntu vantaṉar. tiruvoṟṟiyūril 48 kāvalkārar paṇiyāṟṟi vantaṉar.  (240 of 1912) vicaya nakarattil maṭṭum 12000 nakarak kāvalar iruntaṉarām.
nāṭu eṉṉum perunilap parappi āṇṭu vantavaṉ nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ, taṉ kīḻk kāvalar palarit taliyārikaḷ eṉṟa peyaril niyamittu nāṭu kāval vēliyip pārttu vantāṉ. muṉ coṉṉa muṟiyil vanta nāṭu kāval varumāṉam avaṉic cērntatu. avaṉ attokiyiliruntu taṉ āṭkaḷukkut tiṅkaḷ tōṟum paṇamākavō, nellākavō campaḷam koṭuttu vantāṉ.
perunāṭu pala ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuc ciṟṟaracar palar kiyil iruntu vantatu. ovvoru ciṟṟaracarum tattam nāṭṭikkāval purintaṉar; varuvāyil oru pakuti pēraracaṉukkum oru pakuti taṅkaḷukkum oru pakuti tam kāvalukku uṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭu nalattukkum celavaḻittaṉar. iṅṅaṉam ceytu vantavarkaḷē nāyakkar kālattup pāḷiyakkārarkaḷ.
aṭuttaṭutta iraṇṭu nāṭṭut talivarkaḷ tammuḷ amitiyiṉ poruṭṭu uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytu koḷva tuṇṭu. itaṉāl iru nāṭukaḷilum amitiyē nilaviyirukkum.
nakarattil tiruṭṭō koḷḷiyō naṭappiṉ, ūrkkāvalar kaṭumiyākat taṇṭikkappaṭuvar. oru kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattil oruvaṉ koḷḷi yaṭikkappaṭṭāl, anta iṭam enta nāṭṭic cērntatō, anta nāṭṭuk kāval talivaṉ poruḷi iḻantavaṉukkut taṇṭam koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu vicaya nakara vēntarkaḷ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭiruntaṉar. vicaya nakarattil maṭṭum 12,000 nakarak kāvalar iruntaṉar. ovvoru varukkum mātam 30 paṇam campaḷam koṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. vicayanakara āṭciyil kāvalkārar eṉṟa peyaril ūrkkāvalar iruntaṉar, peru nāṭṭil ciṟappup pakutikaḷil- ellippuṟap pakutikaḷil- aracāṅka nilip paṭikaḷ vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ appakutikaḷil nāṭukāvaṟ paṇiyic ceytu vantaṉar. (South Indian Polity, pp. 244- 247.)

7. paṭi
pōrkkāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1) nāṭu piṭikkum vēṭki pōrukku oru kāraṇamākum.
(2) oru tamiḻaracaṉ maṟṟa iru tamiḻaracari veṉṟu mūṉṟu muṭikaḷit tāṉē cūṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa āvalum pōrukkuriya kāraṇamākum.
(3) ōr aracaṉ makaḷi maṇamuṭikka iyalātu ēmāṟṟam aṭital, aracarkaḷ appeṇṇiṉ nāṭṭiṉmītu pōr toṭuttaṟku oru kāraṇamākum. mūvēntarum paṟampu maliyi muṟṟuki iṭṭataṟku itu kāraṇamākak kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(4) taṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa ciṟṟaracarkaḷ kappaṅkaṭṭat tavaṟiyapōtu aracaṉ pōr puriyavēṇṭiya nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kaliṅkattiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttatu itaṟkākattāṉ.
(5) aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭukaḷukku iṭippaṭṭa nilappakutiyit tam ātikkattil vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa āvalālum pala pōrkaḷ mūṇṭaṉa. kiruṣṇi, tuṅkapattiri āṟukaḷukku iṭippaṭṭa iṭattip paṟṟic cōḻarukkum- cāḷukkiyarukkum, vicaya nakara aracarukkum- pāmiṉi aracarkaḷukkum aṭikkaṭi pōrkaḷ mūṇṭaṉa eṉpatu varalāṟu kaṇṭa uṇmiyākum.
paṭi vaki
	paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭparaṇ āṟum
     uṭiyāṉ aracaruḷ ēṟu
eṉṉum kuṟaḷil paṭi mutaṟkaṇ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal, paṭiyiṉ ciṟappi naṉku uṇarttuvatākum. inta uṇmiyit tamiḻaracarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntiruntaṉar. tolkāppiyam - puṟattiṇi iyalil paṭikaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷum, pōrkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷum naṉkuviḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭi pala pirivukaḷākap pirintu, pala peyarkaḷip peṟṟiruntatu. atu, aṇi, uṇṭi, ōṭṭu eṉappala peyarkaḷ peṟṟatu. mutal varicip paṭikaḷ ākkam, tār (koṭip paṭi), tūci, niri eṉṟum, piṉ varici kūḻi eṉṟum peyarkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. yāṉippaṭi, kutirippaṭi, kālāṭ paṭi eṉṟu paṭi pala pirivukaḷ peṟṟatu.
tēr 
tēr mikac ciṟiyatu; vīraṉum pākaṉum irukkattakka aḷavil ceyyappaṭṭatu. tēril mara vēlippāṭu mikuti. pala niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tērik kutirikaḷ iḻuttuc ceṉṟaṉa. cōḻaṉ uruvap pal tēr iḷañcēṭceṉṉi kutiri pūṭṭap peṟṟa poṉ tēriṉmītu polivōṭu kāṇappaṭṭāṉ eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭiyuḷḷār. (puṟam, ce. 4.) uruvappal tēr eṉṟa toṭar, accōḻaṉiṭam naṉku amikkappeṟṟa pala tērkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatit terivikkiṟatu. cōḻaṉ kurāppaḷḷit tuñciya perun tirumāvaḷavaṉ, ‘kāṟṟu iyaṅkiṉāṟ pōlum tāvutal uṭittākiya katiyi uṭiya kutiriyōṭu koṭi aciyum ucciyi uṭiya tēri uṭiyavaṉ.(puṟam ce. 197.) puṟa nāṉūṟṟil maṭṭum ēṟattāḻa 25 ceyyuṭkaḷil tēr kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tērppākar iruntaṉar. tēr moṭṭu, tērvaṭṭi mutaliya tēriṉ uṟuppukkaḷ kaṭikaḷil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. tērppākar tēr ūrunar eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉar. (cilampu kāti 5, vari 55) ivi aṉittiyum nōkka, caṅka kālattil tērkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teḷivākat terikiṉṟatu. pārivaḷḷal mullikkut tēr īntāṉ eṉpatum iṅku niṉikkat takum. āyiṉ, tērppaṭi eṉṟu oru paṭi iruntamikkuc cāṉṟilli. (tāṉi yāṉi kutiri eṉṟa nōṉār uṭkum mūvaki niliyumeṉpatu tolkāppiyam- puṟattiṇi iyal, cū. 17) 
yāṉippaṭi
cēranāṭu yāṉikaḷukkup peyar pōṉatu. āyvaḷḷal paricilarukku yāṉikaḷiyē paricākak koṭuttāṉ eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu pukalkiṉṟatu. aracaṉ ēṟic ceṉṟa yāṉi neṟṟippaṭṭam uṭiyatu; vēṟu pala āparaṇaṅkaḷi uṭiyatu; tantattil pūṇi uṭiyatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu, 326, 153, 97) yāṉimītu aracaṉatu koṭi kāṟṟil acintāṭik koṇṭiruntatu. aracaṉ yāṉimītu ceṉṟu pakivar kōṭṭik katavukaḷi ataṉ kompukaḷāl kuttac ceyvāṉ. yāṉikaḷ tam kompu kaḷāl kuttik katavukaḷi uṭittaṉa. kaṇiya maratti muṟittaṉa; pōrk kaḷattil pukuntu paki vīraric citaṟaṭittaṉa; paki varuṭiya matilaraṇkaḷi aḻittaṉa.(puṟanāṉūṟu, 3, 6, 9, 13, 31,) cōḻaṉ muṭittalik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi ēṟiyirunta yāṉi emaṉip pōṉṟa tōṟṟamuṭiyatu; kaṭalil cellum kappal pōlavum viṇmīṉkaḷukku iṭiyē cellum muḻumati pōlavum kāṇappaṭṭatu. atu maṟavarkaḷ puṭi cūḻac ceṉṟatu eṉṟu oru ceyyuḷ pēcukiṉṟatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 13)
yāṉippaṭi vīrar vēlkaḷit tāṅkip pōriṭṭaṉar. cīṟūr maṉṉaṉ paṭikaḷi etirttup pōriṭṭa yāṉikaḷ ampukaḷāl tākkuṇṭu tam peṇ yāṉikaḷ nāṇumpaṭi ōṭiṉa. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 308.) neṭuṅkiḷḷikkum nalaṅkiḷḷikkum naṭipeṟṟa pōril yāṉikaḷ paṅku koṇṭaṉa eṉṟu kōvūrkiḻār kuṟittuḷḷār. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 44.) taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṭamum yāṉippaṭi iruntatu eṉṟu mullippāṭṭu moḻikiṉṟatu. pakivaratu kōṭṭik katavukaḷi uṭikkum muyaṟciyil taṉatu kompu oṭintatāl cōḻaṉatu āṇ yāṉi taṉ piṭiyip pārttu veṭkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu muttoḷḷāyiram kūṟukiṉṟatu. caṅka kālattil yāṉiyi velluvatē vīraṉatu kaṭami eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu,
	kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷikkuk kaṭaṉē 				(puṟanāṉūṟu ce.312)
eṉṟa puṟappāṭṭu aṭiyāl teḷivuṟum.
muyalik kuṟi tavaṟāmal eyta ampik kiyil piṭippativiṭa, yāṉi mēl eṟintu tavaṟiya vēlik kiyiṟ piṭittal ciṟantatu eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyuḷḷār. (atikāram, 78, ce. 2.) itaṉāl yāṉiyi eyvatē vīraṉukku aḻaku eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu; kōḻip pōr, kutārip pōr, āṭṭuk kiṭāyp pōr eṉpavi pōla yāṉip pōrum caṅka kālattil naṭi peṟṟatu eṉpatu,
	kuṉṟēṟi yāṉippōr kaṇṭaṟṟāl
eṉṟa kuṟaḷaṭikaḷāl aṟiyalām. (atikāram, 76, ce. 8.)
pallavarkaḷum yāṉippaṭi vittiruntaṉar eṉpatik kūram paṭṭayaṅkaḷum, kāñci vikuntap perumāḷ kōvil ciṟpaṅkaḷum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. cu- ju- kuvā eṉṟa cīṉa yāttirikar cōḻa maṇṭalak kariyilirunta pōr yāṉi kaḷip paṟṟip pēcumiṭattu, aracāṅkattiṭam aṟupatāyiram pōr yāṉikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ uyaram ēḻu allatu eṭṭu aṭi irukkum. ovvoru yāṉiyiṉmītum ōr ampāri uṇṭu. atil vīrar palar iruntukoṇṭu nīṇṭa tolivu variyilum ampukaḷi viṭuvar; pakivari neruṅkiyavuṭaṉ īṭṭikaḷi eṟivar. pōril veṟṟi peṟṟāl, yāṉikaḷukkuc ciṟappup peyar iṭappaṭum, eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷār. (F.N. of S.I. P. 144) oru yāṉi muppatu vīrarkaḷic cumantu celkiṟatu. oru pōr yāṉi ēṟattāḻa 1500 maṉitarkaḷukkuc camameṉṟu collalām. yāṉikaḷiṉ tantaṅkaḷil kūrmiyāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭum, yāṉikaḷ avaṟṟip pakivarmītu pāyccip pakivar paṭikaḷil peruṅkuḻappatti uṇṭākkum, eṉṟu jārṭaṉa eṉṟa ayal nāṭṭār kūṟiyuḷḷār. (F.N. of S.I.g¡. 206.) takkōlap pōril irācātittiyaṉ yāṉimītu iruntu pōriṭṭāṉ. mutalām irācātirācaṉ koppattup pōril yāṉimītiruntu pōr purintāṉ.
kutirip paṭi
caṅkakāla aracar tērkaḷil kāṟṟippōlak kaṭuki ōṭattakka kutirikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu muṉpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. aracaṉukkuriya āṭci maṉṟak kuḻuviṉaruḷ kutirippaṭit talivarum paṅku koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. avarkaḷ ivuḷi maṟavar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. eṉavē, caṅka kālattil paṇpaṭṭa kutirip paṭikaḷ iruntaṉa- appaṭikaḷukkut talivarkaḷ iruntaṉar eṉpaṉa itaṉāl peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa allavā? kutirikaḷ cintu, pāracīkam, arēpiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu mikap palavākat tamiḻakattil iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
pāṇṭiyaṉatu amiccarāṉa maṇivācakar tirupperun tuṟiyil iṟakkumatiyāṉa kutirikaḷi vāṅka aracāṅkap paṇattuṭaṉ tirupperuntuṟikkuc ceṉṟār eṉṟa varalāṟṟuc ceyti, ki.pi. 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum pāṇṭiyar kutirikaḷi vāṅkiṉar eṉpati uṇarttuvatākum.
ki.pi. 13- ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭip pārviyiṭṭa mārkkō pōlō eṉpavar, pāṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭutōṟum iraṇṭāyiram kutirikaḷi vāṅki vantāṉ. ovvoru kappalilum piṟa poruḷkaḷōṭu kutirikaḷum vantapaṭi iruntaṉa. oru kutiriyiṉ vili ēṟattāḻa 400 rūpāy mutal 1200 rūpāy vari iruntatu. tamiḻakattil veppattālum, vaḷarppu muṟit tavaṟṟālum kutirikaḷ aṭikkaṭi nōyuṟutalum, iṟattalumāka iruntaṉa (F.N. of S.I. polity, pp. 166- 168.) eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷār.
kālāṭpaṭi
caṅkakālap pōrvīrar maṟavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. maṟam- vīram. avarkaḷ vil, vēl, vāḷ mutaliya pōrkkaruvikaḷik koṇṭu pōriṭṭaṉar. em ampu kaṭiviṭutum num araṇ cērmiṉ eṉṟa puṟappāṭṭu aṭiyāl, villum ampum iruntami veḷiyākum. avarkaḷ pacukkaḷik kavartal, akaḻi tāṇṭik kōṭṭiyip piṭittal, veṭṭa veḷiyil pōriṭal mutaliya pala tiṟap pōrkaḷil vallunarāy iruntaṉar. avar muṉpaṭi vīrar, piṉpaṭivīrar, tuṇippaṭi vīrar eṉappala peyarkaḷuṭaṉ iruntaṉar.
paṭivīrar taṅkaḷ aracaṉiṭam mikka aṉpum paktiyum koṇṭiruntaṉar; avaṉukkāka uyir viṭavum tuṇintaṉar. pōril muṉ vitta kālip piṉ vikkalākātu- pōril puṟaṅkāṭṭalākātu eṉpatu tamiḻar pōr aṟam. mārpil kāyampaṭṭa vīraṉiyē avaṉ maṉivi pārāṭṭuvāḷ. kuṭumpattiluḷḷa āṭavar aṉivarum pōril iṟantālum akkuṭumpap peṇkaḷ mikka makiḻcci aṭivar; tam āṭavar pōrttiṟaṉip pārāṭṭip pukaḻvar. vīramakaḷir pōrkkaḷam ceṉṟu iṟanta tam kaṇavar uṭalit taḻuvi uyir viṭuvar. iviyellām caṅkakālac ceytikaḷ.
caṅkakāla vīrarkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṉittum tolkāppiyam- puṟattiṇi iyal, puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟup pattu, cilappatikāram ivaṟṟil parakkak kāṇalām.
tamiḻaracar mukkiyamāṉa ellippakutikaḷil kōṭṭi kaḷi amittu, aṅku nilippaṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉar. pēraracarkku aṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracar tam aḷavukku ēṟṟapaṭi paṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉar; uṇavu, uṭi, ūtiyam ivaṟṟi vīrarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉar. 
piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil nāṭṭuppaṭi, kikkōḷap perumpaṭi, vēḷikkārappaṭi eṉap palavāṟu paṭikaḷukkup peyarkaḷ amintiruntaṉa. aracāṅkap paṭiyil pala pirivukaḷ iruntaṉa. paṇpaṭṭa vīrar paṭiyē kik kōḷap perum paṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. mutalām irācarācaṉ kālattil paṭiyil 31 pirivukaḷ iruntaṉa.  (S.I. Ins. II, Insp. 9.)
vēḷikkārappaṭi eṉpatu, aracaṉukku āpattu varum vēḷiyilum, nāṭṭukku āpattu varum vēḷiyilum tam uyiriyum matiyāmal pōr puriyum vīrarkaḷik koṇṭatu. tēvippaṭṭa pōtu vantu utavi puriyum nilippaṭi itu  eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. iṉṉatu ceyyattavaṟiṉāl iṉṉavāṟu maṭivōm eṉṟu capatam ceytu koṇṭa vīrarē vēḷikkārar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivaruḷ cilar ūrkaḷip pātukkāttaṉar; cilar tiruccūla vēḷikkārar eṉṟa peyaril kōvilkaḷik kaṇkāṇittaṉar. vēḷikkārap paṭiyiṉar pāṇṭiyaṉiṭam āpattutavikaḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉar.  (391, 395 of 1917; 532, 433 of 1917.)
cōḻar kālattil maḷiyāḷap paṭi, vaṭukar paṭi eṉpaṉavum iruntaṉa. cātāraṇa kālattilum nāṭṭip pātukākka aminta paṭiyē nāṭṭuppaṭi eṉpatu. ain nūṟṟuvar eṉṟār pōṉṟa vaṇikar caṅkaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup paṭikaḷi vittiruntaṉa. vīrarkaḷukku viṭappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷ allatu varuvāy amaram eṉappaṭṭatu. (S.I. polity pp. 259- 261. )
paṭikkaruvikaḷ
caṅka kālattil vēl, vāḷ, vil ampu mutaliyaṉa ciṟanta pōrkkaruvikaḷāka iruntaṉa. maturikkōṭṭi matilmītu kīḻvarum pōrkkaruvikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu.; vaḷintu tāṉē eyyum iyantira vil, kariyaviral uṭiya kuraṅku pōl iruntu cērntārik kaṭikkum poṟi, kalli umiḻum kavaṇ, kāyntu iṟittalāl cērntāri varuttum ney, cempi urukkum miṭā, urukkāycci eṟivataṟku eḵku paṭṭirukkum ulikaḷ, kal iṭṭu vikkum kūṭi, tūṇṭil vaṭivākac ceytu viṭappaṭṭu vittu matilēṟum etirikaḷikkōttu valikkum karuvi, kaḻukkōl pōlak kaḻuttil pūṭṭi muṟukkum caṅkali, āṇṭalippuḷ vaṭivākap paṇṇip paṟakkaviṭa ucciyikkotti muḷiyik kaṭikkum poṟi varicikaḷ, matilmītu ēṟuvōri maṟiyat taḷḷum iruppukkavi, kaḻukkōl, ampukkaṭṭu, ēvaṟikaḷ, ciṟṟampukaḷ vittu eyyum iyantiram, matiliṉ ucciyip piṭippavar kikaḷikkuttum ūcip poṟikaḷ, pakivarmēl ceṉṟu kaṇṇikkottum ciccilippoṟi, matil ucciyil ēṟiṉavar uṭalik kompāl kiḻikka irumpāl ceytu vitta paṉṟip poṟi, mūṅkil vaṭivākappaṇṇi aṭippataṟku amitta poṟi, katavukku valimiyāka uḷ vāyiṟpaṭiyil nilattil viḻaviṭum maraṅkaḷ, kaṉiyamaram, viṭṭēṟu, kuntam, īṭṭi, nūṟṟuvarikkolli, taḷḷiveṭṭi, kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, viḻuṅkum pāmpu, kaḻukup poṟi, pulippoṟi, kuṭappāmku cakaṭappoṟi, takarppoṟi, ñāyil (kuruvittali). (kāti 15, vari 207- 217; uri.)
vicayanakara vēntar kālattil veṭimaruntum pīraṅkikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
peruvaḻikaḷ
pōrukku puṟappaṭumuṉ aracaṉ taṉatu palam, paṇam, nāṭu, kālam, māṟṟāṉ palam mutaliyavaṟṟi ārāyvatu vaḻakkam; araciyal tūtuvarik koṇṭum oṟṟarik koṇṭum pakivaruṭiya paṭivaṉmiyi aṟivatu vaḻakkam: paṭiyeṭukku muṉpu, kīḻppaṭiyuṅkaḷ; iṉṟēl, uṅkaḷ nāṭu aḻiyum, eṉṟu pakivarukku eccarikki viṭutal marapu.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉ paṭikaḷuṭaṉ imayamali ceṉṟāṉ. - karikālaṉ imayamali ceṉṟāṉ eṉpavaṟṟi nōkka, caṅkakālattil intiyāvil peruvaḻikaḷ (Highways)     iruntaṉa eṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu vaṭa nāṭṭiṟkuc cella antira (vaṭuka) nāṭṭiṟku mēṟkē peruvaḻi oṉṟu iruntatu. atu vaḻivaḻi mēṟku eṉṟu collappaṭṭatu. kaṭappi, karnūl, aṉantapūr māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya nilap pakutikkuk kiḻakkē ivvaṭuka vaḻi mēṟku amintiruntatu. annilap pakutiyip pāṇar (Banas)  āṇṭu vantaṉar. ki.pi.3- ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mayūracarmaṉ eṉṟa katampar kula mutalvaṉ nāṭu piṭikkat toṭaṅkiya piṉpu pāṇa aracarkaḷ vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar. (Epigraphia Indica, Xi. p. 231; S.I. I. III. p.90.) ipperuvaḻi caṅka kālattilēyē iruntatu eṉpataṟku icceytiyē ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum.
piṟkālac cōḻar kālattil kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻaṉatu paṭi kaṅki variyil ceṉṟu mīṇṭatu. mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ paṭi kaliṅkam variyil ceṉṟu mīṇṭatu. cōḻar paṭikaḷ tuṅkapattiri āṟṟukku appālum ceṉṟu pōriṭṭaṉa eṉṟa ceytikaḷi nōkka, piṟkālac cōḻar kālattilum nāṭṭil peruvaḻikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu tēṟṟam.
oru nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭivīrar cellum poḻutu avarkaḷōṭu camiyaṟkārar, calavit toḻilāḷarkaḷ taṇṇīr tarupavar, irumpuk kolvar, paṇi makkaḷ, paṇip peṇkaḷ mutaliyōr celvatu vaḻakkam. vīrarkaḷukku uḷḷak kiḷarcciyi uṇṭākka āṭal- pāṭal makaḷirum uṭaṉ celvar. (S.I. polity, p. 268)
pōr vaki
oru nāṭṭiṉmēl paṭiyeṭukka virumpum aracaṉ mutalil taṉ vīrari ēvip pakivaruṭiya kālnaṭikaḷik kavarvāṉ. itu veṭcit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. im muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭavar veṭci malarkaḷic cūṭuvar. immuyaṟciyi etirttu niṟpatu karantit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. avarkaḷ karanti malarkaḷic cūṭik koḷvar. piṉpu annāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuppu naṭipeṟum. atu vañcit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. appaṭiyeṭuppi etirttu niṟṟal kāñcit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. kōṭṭiyi muṟṟukiyiṭal uḻiñit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. kōṭṭiyuḷ iruntu etirttal noccit tiṇi eṉappaṭum.  kōṭṭikkappāl veṭṭa veḷiyil naṭi peṟum kaṭum pōr tumpit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. pōril peṟṟi peṟutal vākit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. ovvoru tiṇic ceyalilum īṭupaṭṭa vīrarkaḷ attiṇikkuriya malarkaḷ aṇintu koḷḷutal marapu.
mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa ovvoru tiṇippōrum pala paṭikaḷiyuṭiyatu. ap paṭikaḷ tuṟikaḷ eṉappaṭum. ippōrkkali paṟṟiya viḷakkam tolkāppiyam- puṟattiṇi iyalil teḷivākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkali vaḷarcci tolkāppiyar kālattil tiṭīreṉa ēṟpaṭṭiruttal iyalātu. tolkāppiyarkku muṉṉarē - pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka vaḷarntu vanta pōrk kaliyiṉ virivē tolkāppiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu koḷḷutalē aṟivuṭimiyākum.
aracaṉ allatu avaṉ nampikkikkuriya paṭit talivaṉ paṭikaḷukkut talimit tāṅkip pōrukkuc cellutal vaḻakkam. karikālaṉ, cēraṉ, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pōṉṟōr tāmē paṭikaḷukkut talimi tāṅkiṉar. kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻaṉ kālattil iḷavaracaṉ irācātirācaṉē paṭikaḷukkut talimi tāṅkiṉāṉ.
pācaṟi nikaḻccikaḷ
pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉatu pācaṟi iravu nikaḻccikaḷ kīḻvarumāṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv vivaraṅkaḷi liruntu pācaṟi nikaḻccikaḷi oruvāṟu nām aṟiyalām:
“pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉakkeṉṟaminta kūṭārattil tiric cīli toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa uḷḷaṟiyil uḷḷa paṭukkiyil oru kiyi vittum maṟṟoru kiyi muṭiyuṭaṉ cērttum irukkiṟāṉ; mutal nāṭpōril yāṉiyi vēlāl eṟintu iṟanta vīrari niṉikkiṟāṉ; ampu titta tuṉpattāl cevi cāyttup pul uṇṇāmal niṟkum parikaḷi niṉikkiṟāṉ; ‘ippaṭi ivvaḷavu taḷarcciyuṟṟiruppatāl, itaṉik koṇṭu nām nāḷi veṟṟi kāṇpatu eṅṅaṉam?  eṉat taḷarcci aṭikiṟāṉ.
avvamayam aṅkuk kālattiṉ aḷavi aḷantu colluvōr maṉṉaṉip paṇintu vāḻtti, nāḻiki vaṭṭiliṟ ceṉṟa nāḻiki ittuṇi eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu tūya veḷḷiyāṭiyi uṭutta aḻakikaḷ viḷakkik koḷuttukiṉṟaṉar.
pācaṟiyil nāṉku terukkaḷ kūṭum ovvoru cannitiyilum oru yāṉi kāvalāka niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aru karumpiyum, neṟkatirōṭu kalantu kaṭṭiya iliyiyum, atimaturat taḻiyiyum uṇṇavilli; avaṟṟāl taṉatu neṟṟiyit tuṭikkiṟatu; avaṟṟit tutikkiyil ēntit tutikkiyit taṉ kompiṉmītu vitta vaṇṇam niṟkiṉṟatu. pākaṉ, yāṉip pēccukkaḷic collip piḷavupaṭṭa parikkōlāl kavaḷatti uṇṇumpaṭik kuttukiṟāṉ. iṭuppil vāḷ aṇinta peṇ vēliyāṭkaḷ ney niṟinta tirikkuḻiyil nīṇṭa tiriyik koṇṭu cevviyāka aminta viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟukiṉṟaṉar; viḷakkukaḷ aviyum poḻutu tam kiyil uḷḷa pantattik koṇṭu avaṟṟik koḷuttukiṉṟaṉar.
caṭṭiyum talippākiyum aṇinta meykāppāḷar aracaṉatu iruppic cuṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. caṭṭiyiṭṭa ūmar aracaṉ paḷḷiyaṟiyik kāval ceykiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉō, maṟunāḷ evvāṟu pōr ceyyalām eṉpatip paṟṟi eṇṇiya vaṇṇam tuyil koḷḷātirukkiṉṟāṉ. (ṭākṭar mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār, pattuppāṭṭuk kāṭcikaḷ, pak . 47- 48. (paḻaniyappā piratar, tiruccirāppaḷḷi))
kōṭṭiyum muṟṟukiyum
kōṭṭikaḷ tariyil kaṭṭappaṭṭavi, taṇṇīrāl cūḻappaṭṭavi, malimītu kaṭṭappaṭṭavi, kāṭukaḷukku iṭiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭavi eṉṟu nāṉku vakippaṭum. nāṭṭiṉ ellippuṟaṅkaḷil akkālattil kāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. ellippuṟak kōṭṭikaḷ paṭikaḷ taṅki iruppataṟkākavē amikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi paṭippaṟṟu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. (394 of 1921) ittakiya kōṭṭikaḷiṉ celavukkākak kōṭṭip paṇam eṉṟa vari vacūlikkappaṭṭatu. (Travancore Archeacological Series, V.P. 205.) uḷnāṭṭilum mukkiyamāṉa pakutikaḷil ittakiya kōṭṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. caṅka kālattil paṟampu mali mūvēntarāl muṟṟuki iṭappaṭṭatu. cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi- neṭuṅkiḷḷikaḷiṉ pōril āvūrkkōṭṭiyum, uṟiyūrk kōṭṭiyum muṟṟukiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. im muṟṟukit toṭarpāṉa vivaraṅkaḷip puṟanāṉūṟṟil kāṇalām.
muṟṟukiyiṭupavar uḻiñiyār eṉappaṭuvar. muṟṟuki yiṭappaṭupavar nocciyār eṉappaṭuvar. ivvirutiṟattārum ceyta pōrvakikaḷ tolkāppiyattilum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyilum viḷakkamākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vicayanakaram ēḻu matilkaḷik koṇṭiruntatu. kōṭṭikkuḷḷiruntavar vacatiyāka irukkap paḻaṅkālattil ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ maturik kōṭṭiyi aḻittāṉ, māṟavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ tañcāvūrk kōṭṭiyiyum uṟiyūrk kōṭṭiyiyum aḻittāṉ eṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. caṅka kālattil kōṭṭik katavukaḷ yāṉikaḷāl tākkappaṭṭaṉa- yāṉikaḷ taṅkaḷ tantaṅkaḷāl tākkiṉa eṉṟa ceytikaḷ muṉṉarē kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa allavā?
kaṭaṟpaṭi
tamiḻakam pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākak kaṭal vāṇikattil ciṟantiruntatu. eṉavē, mūṉṟu tamiḻaracarkaḷum kaṭaṟpaṭiyi vittiruntaṉar eṉpatu poruntum. kappalkaḷi vaḻimaṟittuk koḷḷiyaṭittu oru tīvil vāḻntu vanta kaṭampari neṭuñcēralātaṉ veṉṟāṉ; avvāṟē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum veṉṟāṉ; kaṭalpiṟak kōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ. ivvivaraṅkaḷip patiṟṟuppattil kāṇalām.
pallavar kālattil mutalām naracimmavarmaṉ māmalla purattiliruntu kaṭaṟpaṭiyi ilaṅkikku iraṇṭu muṟi aṉuppiṉāṉ. (S.I. I. II. P. 356.) irācacimmaṉ ilaṭcat tīvukaḷi veṉṟāṉ. (E. I. 18 .p. 152.) icceytikaḷ pallavar kaṭaṟpaṭi vittiruntaṉar eṉṟa uṇmiyit terivikkiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālac cōḻariṭam periya kaṭaṟpaṭi iruntatu. akkaṭaṟpaṭiyiṉ tuṇiyāl mutalām irācarācaṉ ilaṅkiyiyum munnīrppaḻantīvu paṉṉīrā yirattiyum veṉṟāṉ; kaṅkikoṇṭa cōḻaṉ malēyā, kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ, nikōpart tīvukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi veṉṟāṉ.
aracar vañciṉam
pōrukkuc cellumuṉ aracar cilar cūḷ urittal vaḻakkam. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ imayamali vari paṭi yeṭuttuc cellumpōtu cūḷ ceytāṉ eṉpatic cilappatikārattil kāṇalām. (kāti 25, vari 128- 140.) maturiyi āṇṭa pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ, eṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu vanta pakivari vellēṉāyiṉ, eṉ maṉiviyiviṭṭu yāṉ pirivēṉāka; aṟamnili peṟṟuḷḷa aviyil (nīti maṉṟattil) aṟamaṟṟa oruvaṉi vittu muṟi kalaṅkik koṭuṅkōl ceytēṉ ākuka. miyal eṉṉum ūrkkut talivaṉākiya māvaṉ, eyil eṉṉum ūrukkut talivaṉākiya ānti, antuvaṉ cāttaṉ, ātaṉ aḻici, iyakkaṉ eṉṉum eṉ naṇparatu makiḻ nakiyit tappiyavaṉ ākuka; pala uyirkaḷiyum pātukākkum pāṇṭiya araca marapiliruntu nīṅkip pakivaruṭiya vaṉpulaṅkaḷik kākkum kāvalāḷiyākap piṟappēṉākuka, eṉac cūḷurittāṉ. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 71.)
taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, ippakivari yāṉ vellēṉāyiṉ, eṉṉik koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ eṉṟu eṉ kuṭikaḷ tūṟṟuvarāka. māṅkuṭi marutaṉ pōṉṟa pulavar perumakkaḷ eṉatu nāṭṭippāṭātu oḻivarāka,” eṉṟu cūḷ ceytāṉ.(puṟam, ce. 72.) cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi, ip pakivari yāṉ vellēṉāyiṉ, eṉatu māli potu makaḷir mārpil tuvaḷvatāka, eṉṟu cūḷ urittāṉ. (puṟam. ce. 73.)
pōr nikaḻccikaḷ
karikālaṉ pakivar nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. avaṉ kippaṟṟiya ūrkaḷil matilkaḷ aḻintaṉa. kūkikaḷ iruntu kuḻaṟiṉa eṉṟu paṭṭiṉappāli kūṟukiṉṟatu. (vari 239- 260) paṭiyeṭukkum aracaṉic cērnta vīrar pakivar nāṭṭu ūrkaḷik koḷuttutal vaḻakkam: kōṭṭikaḷi iṭippatu vaḻakkam; inta aḻivukaḷi ellām puṟanāṉūṟṟil kāṇalām. piṟkāla aḻivukaḷik kaliṅkattup paraṇiyil virivākak kāṇalām. nī paṭiyeṭuttāl pakivar nāṭu aḻivuṟumē eṉṟu pulavarkaḷ varuntip pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ pala. ittakiya pāṭalkaḷ koṟṟa vaḷḷi eṉṟu peyar peṟum. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 4, 7, 41, 98) aracaṉatu talinakaril kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ iṭikkappaṭum; anta nilam kaḻutikaḷāl uḻappaṭum; kavaṭi vitikkappaṭum. veṉṟa aracar vellap paṭṭa nāṭṭuk kuḷaṅkaḷil tam yāṉikaḷi nīrāṭṭuvar; veṉṟa nāṭṭil taṅkaḷ veṟṟit tūṇkaḷi nāṭṭuvar, allatu taṅkaḷ ilacciṉiyip poṟippar; veṉṟa aracar tōṟṟa aracar kuṭiyiliruntu tam kālukkuriya kaḻalic ceytu koḷvar; vīrakaṅkaṇamum ceytu koḷvar. ivi caṅka nūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟum ceytikaḷ.
veṉṟa aracaṉ pōrkkaḷattil ceyyum vēḷvi kaḷavēḷvi eṉappaṭum. veṉṟa aracaṉ tōṟṟa aracaṉ maṉiviyaruṭiya talimayirik kayiṟākki ataṉit taṉ tēri iḻukkap payaṉpaṭuttuvatum uṇṭu. tōṟṟa aracaṉuṭiya paṟkaḷik kōṭṭik katavukaḷil patiya vittalum uṇṭu. veṟṟi peṟṟa maṉṉaruḷ cilar iṅṅaṉam kīḻttaramāka naṭattalum uṇṭu. ivi paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷip puṟanāṉūṟṟilum pattuppāṭṭilum patiṟṟuppattilum kāṇalām.
pōr vīraruṭ cilar tamatu taṉippōr muṟiyāl ciṟappup peṟutalum uṇṭu. attakiya vīraṉ pōril uyir viṭṭāl, aracaṉum piṟarum avaṉ niṉivāka vīrakkal naṭṭu vaḻipāṭu ceyvar. vīrar mukattil allatu mārpil  puṇpaṭṭāl veṭkappaṭuvar; avaṟṟik kiḻittu virivil iṟantupaṭuvar. itu maṟak kāñci eṉappaṭum. (vākippaṭalam, veṇpā 22.) pōril iṟantupaṭṭa vīrar uṭalkaḷit taḻuvi avartam maṉiviyar pōrkkaḷattilēyē uyir viṭuvar. oru tāy taṉ orē makaṉip pōrkkōlam koḷḷac ceytu pōrukku aṉuppiṉāḷ. avaḷuṭiya tanti, tamiyaṉ mār, kaṇavaṉ ākiyōr muṉpu naṭipeṟṟa pōrkaḷil iṟantaṉar. iṟutippōril makaṉum iṟantāṉ. avaṉ uṭal muḻuvatiyum ampukaḷ tikkappeṟṟiruntatik kaṇṭa tāy makiḻccik kaṇṇīr viṭṭāḷ eṉṟu puṟap poruḷ veṇpāmāli kūṟukiṉṟatu. (tol. puṟattiṇiyiyal, cū. 79.)
pōrkkaḷattil uyirviṭṭa  vīraṉukkāka avaṉ kuṭumpattārukku nilaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu uṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkutal utirappaṭṭi (utiram- irattam) eṉappaṭum. (47of 1928...29, A.R.E. 1929, Para 34.) ciṟappu muṟiyil tam āṟṟali veḷippaṭuttiya vīrarkaḷ ēṉāti, mārāyaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟaṉar.
paṭi amippu
aracaṉuṭiya yāṉip paṭikku oru periya talivaṉ uṇṭu. ivvāṟē ovvoru paṭikkum periya talivaṉ iruntāṉ. ovvoru paṭiyilum pala uṭpirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ovvōr uṭpirivukaḷukkum oru ciṟiya talivaṉ iruntāṉ. ippalavakip paṭikaḷum cērnta amippukkup perun talivaṉ oruvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ cēṉātipati taṇṭa nāyakkaṉ, makātaṇṭa nāyakkaṉ, taḷavāy eṉap pala kālaṅkaḷil palavāṟu peyar peṟṟāṉ. avaṉukku aracāṅka virutukaḷum mariyātikaḷum uṇṭu. avaṉ oru ciṟṟaracaṉukkuriya takutiyil vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ cila camayaṅkaḷil mutalamiccaṉākavum iruntāṉ. mutalām irācēntiraṉ pōṉṟa iḷavaracarkaḷē cila kālaṅkaḷil appatavikaḷil iruntaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil taṇṭa nāyakappatavi maṭṭum taṉittu iruntatu.
pēraracaṉ paṭikaḷ
pēraracaṉatu paṭi payiṟcippeṟṟa paṭiyākum. ataṉ cila pakutikaḷ perunāṭṭiṉ ellip puṟaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka iṭaṅkaḷil kōṭṭikaḷi amittukkoṇṭu niliyāka iruntu vantaṉa. cila paṭikaḷ uḷnāṭṭup pakutikaḷil irunta kōṭṭikaḷil āṅkāṅku iruntu vantaṉa. cila pakutikaḷ tali nakarattil iruntaṉa. maṟṟum cila pakutikaḷ nāṭu kāval mutaliya paṇikaḷil kuṟikaḷ nērāmal pārttu vantaṉa. ippaṭi kaḷellām paṇpaṭṭavi.
ciṟṟaracarum paṭikaḷum
pēraracukku uṭpaṭṭa ciṟṟaracarkaḷ palar iruntaṉar. ovvoru ciṟṟaracaṉiṭamum paṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. pēraracaṉukkut tēvippaṭum poḻutu ciṟṟaracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ paṭikaḷi utavavēṇṭum eṉpatu viti. āṉāl appaṭi vīrar paṇpaṭṭavarallar. avaruṭ palar pōrkkālattil maṭṭum vīrarāka iruppar; eñciya kālaṅkaḷil payirt toḻil pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷil īṭupaṭṭiruppar, ittakiya vīrarik koṇṭa paṭikaḷāl ciṟanta muṟiyil pōr ceytal iyalātataṉṟō? teṉṉintiyappaṭi vīrar pōrttuṟiyil kuṟiyuṭiyavar eṉpataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷil itu ciṟappāṉa tākum.
pōrkkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷi makiḻvikka āṭal pāṭal makaḷir uṭaṉ celvar. avarkaḷuṭiya āṭal pāṭalkaḷ vīrarkku makiḻcciyi ūṭṭiṉum, vēṟu niṉivukaḷiyum ūṭṭi vantaṉa. eṉavē, immuṟiyum pōrttiṟami paḻutaṭi vataṟku maṟṟoru kāraṇamāka amintatu.
ciṟṟaracarkaḷ tammuḷ aṭikkaṭi pōr purintaṉar; pēraracaṉukku aṭaṅkāmalum cila camayaṅkaḷil tirintaṉar; valimiyaṟṟa pēraracaṉ āṭciyil ciṟṟaracarkaḷ nāṭu perukkuvatil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. mūṉṟām irācarācaṉ āṭciyil ciṟṟaracar tam valimiyip perukkik koṇṭaṉar. cēnta maṅkalatti āṇṭa kōpperuñciṅkaṉ taṉ pēraracaṉākiya mūṉṟām irācarācaṉiyē ciṟippaṭuttiṉāṉ. ciṟṟaracarkaḷip pōlavē avartam paṭittalivarkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil oṟṟumiyiṉṟip pūcaliṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. ittakiya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl tamiḻppaṭi vīrar piṟkālaṅkaḷil valimi iḻantaṉar; ataṉāl mulimkaḷ paṭiyeṭutta pōtu puṟaṅkoṭuttaṉar.
mulim vīrarkaḷ kaṭṭuppāṭu uṭiyavarkaḷ; ciṟanta muṟiyil pōrppayiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷ, avar tam kaṭṭuppāṭu intu vīrarkaḷukku amiyavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē kiruṣṇatēvarāyar mulim vīrarkaḷik koṇṭa paṭioṉṟi amittār. appaṭi toṭarntu iruntu vantatu. (S.I. polity,pp, 292- 293)
ayal nāṭṭut toṭarpu
ovvoru nāṭṭilum ellāp poruḷkaḷum kiṭippa tilli. ellā nāṭukaḷilum ellāk kittoḻilkaḷum naṭipeṟutalum illi. ātalāl oru nāṭu piṟanāṭukaḷōṭu naṭpukkoṇṭu taṉakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷip peṟuvatē iyalpu; annāṭukaḷil illāta - taṉṉiṭattil maṭṭum uḷḷa- poruḷkaḷi aṅku aṉuppuvatum iyalpu. inta muṟiyil tāṉ uḷ nāṭṭu vāṇikamum veḷināṭṭu vāṇikamum tōṉṟiṉa. ēṟakkuṟiya aiyāyiram āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻnāṭu ayal nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikat toṭarpik koṇṭiruntatu. vāṇikat toṭarpāl nākarikat toṭarpum paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpum uṇṭātal iyaṟki.
tamiḻakam kiḻakkil uḷḷa cīṉam, malēyā, jāvā mutaliya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉum, mēṟkil rōm, kirī, ekiptu, ciṟiya āciyā, arēpiyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu. hīpru moḻi nūlkaḷil collappaṭṭa tuki eṉpatum, tamiḻt tōki eṉpatum cālamaṉ (ki.mu. 1000) kappalkaḷiṟ tamiḻakattup poruḷkaḷ ceṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, ki.mu. 3000 kkuk kuṟiyāta kālattil tēkku marat tuṇṭaṅkaḷ arēpiya nāṭṭiṟkut tamiḻakattiliruntu aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum tamiḻakattuk kaṭalvāṇikap paḻimiyi uṇarttum cāṉṟukaḷākum. (Caldwell, comp. gr.p. 88; Regozin, Vedic India. p . 305.) aṇmiyil uḷḷa ilaṅkittīvuṭaṉ araciyal, vāṇikam, paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟil toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu. muriyar kālattil vaṭanāṭṭuṭaṉ vāṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu.
aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ aṇmi nāṭṭu aracarkaḷip perumpālum aiyakkaṇ koṇṭē pārttu vantaṉar. oruvari oruvar nampuvatilli. ataṉāl ellip puṟaṅkaḷi eppoḻutum vīrarkaḷ kāval kāttu vantaṉar. maṇṇāciyē perumpālāṉa pōrkaḷukku aṭippaṭik kāraṇam. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar orē moḻi pēci, orē nākarikattil vaḷarntavar. āyiṉum, avaruḷ oṟṟumi illi; cōḻaṉ kurāppaḷḷit tuñciya peruntiru māvaḷavaṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻutiyum oruṅku iruntaṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷik kaṇṭu makiḻnta kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār,
	ētil makkaḷ potumoḻi koḷḷā(tu)
	 iṉṟē pōlkanum puṇarcci
eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttiṉār. (puṟanāṉūṟu, ce. 58.)
